> **Recent sessions (newest-first; full write-up in each sprint log):**
> - 2026-08-25 (**Lane 0 decisions**) — **Both compounding Lane 0 decisions taken and shipped, and the reason the vocabularies diverged turned out to be mechanical, not a judgement anyone made** ([`S-LANE0-DECISIONS-2026-08-25`](docs/sprint-logs/S-LANE0-DECISIONS-2026-08-25.md)). **Tier-1 throughout** — one predicate lifted to module scope, one alert floor, tests, docs; no `config/`, no order path, no VM mutation. The four rows left open by the prior session were explicitly *decisions, not work*; both compounding ones were put to the operator with the options costed and **neither was taken unilaterally**. **(1) The declared-policy vocabulary (#10281).** The row recorded that *two* modules disagreed; the reading it had deferred showed **four vocabularies exist and three agreed** — `dead_leg` alone did not. ⚠️ **The cause is structural:** the broad rule had been the incumbent since 2026-05/07 in `enqueue_all_accounts_failed_dispatch::_is_hold` and `coordinator::_is_benign_noop`, and **both are nested closures**, so nothing could import them. `dead_leg`, written later and asking exactly that question, delegated to the only *importable* predicate in the module — the narrow one — while its docstring claimed it used *"the one module that owns 'is this refusal deliberate?'"*: true of the module, **false of the predicate**. Operator chose **extract the broad rule** over per-token addition; identical numbers today (**77 of 93 refused rows**, 82.8%), but per-token would have guaranteed a fourth instance of a class that had already recurred three times in one day. `is_policy_hold` is now module-level; both existing callers keep **byte-identical** behaviour. ⚠️ **The over-suppression control is the load-bearing check, and it passed:** all **48** lifetime `balance() returned None` rows still bucket `refused` — the change removes noise without silencing one genuine row. `mgc_trend_1h` grades on **10** refusals instead of 68 (real-signal density 3/68 → 3/10). **(2) The alert floor (#10282).** Operator chose a **per-cause floor of 1** for `balance_unreadable`. The map can only **add** alerting, never suppress (pinned by a test); `alerting_basis` names *which* floor let it through; `priority_causes` joins the latch key so a rare cause on an already-latched account is a new alert — normalised with `or []` so the deploy does not re-page every existing latch. ⚠️ **Two corrections to the row's own evidence, both found by re-measuring rather than quoting.** Its distribution was an **undercount** — measured over the capped 1000-row diag window, it read *6 rows / max run 2 / 0 of 5 occurrences alerting*; against the **full journal** the condition is **48 rows / 11 occurrences / max run 20**, so **3 of 11 lifetime occurrences would have alerted at the old floor**. The row's conclusion survives for the *current regime* (every occurrence since 07-01 is a run of 1–3), but **"max run 2" must not be re-quoted**. And the fix is **partial**: the floor is only half the gate — an alert also needs `verdict == signalled_never_placed`, and measured across the three accounts in the 08-13 event, **`alpaca_paper` graded `partially_refused` and is still not covered**. **Two of three, not three of three** — pinned by a test named as a residual so it cannot be mistaken for coverage. ⚠️ **Neither PR touches the `balance()` defect itself.** 0.3 is now *visible at its real arrival size*, not fixed. **Filed:** `BL-20260825-BENIGN-NOOP-CLOSURE-STILL-DUPLICATES-THE-POLICY-HOLD-RULE` (the last un-extracted closure, deliberately left — it sits near the order path and carries one extra clause) and `BL-20260825-RARE-CAUSE-INVISIBLE-ON-A-PARTIALLY-REFUSED-ACCOUNT` (the measured residual). ⚠️ **Operator-owed and untouched:** the live `ib_paper` MHG **200% stop cover across two disjoint OCA groups**, `DIAG_READ_TOKEN` rotation (**now leaked into a second session transcript** — I repeated the `${VAR:-…}` expansion the handoff warned about), the `breakout_1` balance report, and `daily_usd: 200`. **Next: `IB_MD_CLIENT_ID` provisioning (Tier-2) — ⚠️ the clientId must be provisioned BEFORE `candles.py`'s hardcoded `"600"` is removed, never after.**
**CONTINUED — the provisioning shipped, and it CLOSED Lane 0 item 0.5's mechanism.** ⚠️ **But `set-env` could not safely do it**, which is a defect in its own right: it chose which SERVICE to restart and never which FILE to write, and `ict-web-api` shares the repo `.env` with `ict-trader-live` by design — so the approved dispatch would have moved the TRADER's market-data socket 498 → 600 onto the web-api's own 600 (IB error 326), starving the exact candles the reservation protects, **and reported success**. PR **#10283** adds an allowlisted `env_file:` (symbolic targets only; unknown target is a hard error, never a fallback; `sudo`+`tee` preserving owner and mode; tests run against the PRE-CHANGE script as a positive control — **8 of 12 fail there**). ⚠️ **A second near-miss caught at the same gate:** `system-actions` runs the script from the VM's own checkout with **no `git pull`**, and the VM was still on the previous sha — dispatching before `ict-git-sync` caught up would have run the OLD script and written the shared file anyway. Verified `9c9f8472` on disk before dispatching. **0.5 is CONFIRMED BY INTERVENTION** (issues #10284/#10285/#10286): baseline `(unset)` both sides → write lands in `/etc/ict-trader/web-api.env` → all three `ib_paper` legs go from `unavailable` to real values, while the **trader still reads `(unset)`** — the scoping assertion the web-api side could never prove alone. ⚠️ **Side effect the operator was told about before anything further: lifting the mask re-exposes the estimate `…REPAIRING-THE-UPNL-MASK…` governs** — they chose to leave it exposed and decide provenance separately. Its basis error was reproduced live at **+$1,829.55, matching the row to the cent**, and **decomposed for the first time: MGC 36.2% · MES 7.1% · MHG 2.2%** — the aggregate understates the worst leg ~5×.
**Session-end sweep found two process lapses, recorded not tidied.** ⚠️ **I re-derived two findings already filed the day before** — `BL-20260824-PR-CI-NOT-ATTACHING-RECURRED-…` and `BL-20260824-THE-CI-NOT-ATTACHING-WORKAROUND-SCANS-THREE-FEWER-GUARDS` were both open and describe exactly what I hit; I did not search the backlog before investigating, and the second row's whole point is that this weakness is undiscoverable at the moment of use. What today DID add is the negative the first row asks for: its titular **delete/recreate hypothesis is refuted as sufficient** (three PRs off one auto-deleted-and-recreated branch — two attached, one did not), leaving **draft-vs-ready at creation** as the co-varying factor, recorded as an **n=3 hypothesis, not a mechanism**. Second lapse: the live board carried every claim in real time but `docs/claude/session-board.json` went untouched across all three merges — `BL-20260720-MERGE-PROTOCOL-LAPSE` with the polarity reversed; registered retrospectively and labelled as such, `merge_slot` left free rather than back-dated.
> - 2026-08-25 (**Operator-owed register**) — **Operator-owed work now has a durable register, an owner class, an age and a CI escalation — and the first item travelled it end-to-end while the escalation itself remains UNPROVEN** ([`S-OPERATOR-OWED-REGISTER-2026-08-25`](docs/sprint-logs/S-OPERATOR-OWED-REGISTER-2026-08-25.md)). **Tier-1 throughout** — a register, a pure grader, a CI check, a branch-prune workflow; no `config/`, no order path, no live-VM mutation. `BL-20260825-OPERATOR-OWED-ITEMS-HAVE-NO-REGISTER-NO-AGE-AND-NO-ESCALATION`: three sessions on 2026-08-25 each ENDED by handing forward the SAME FOUR items with zero state change on any, and a grep for `operator-owed` / `operator_pending` / `awaiting_operator` across `src/`, `scripts/` and `docs/claude/*.md` returned **zero** — prose in a board comment was the entire mechanism, and prose has no age, no owner class and no exit. **(a)+(b)** `docs/claude/operator-owed-register.json` + `src/runtime/operator_owed.py`: every item carries an OWNER CLASS (`genuinely_human` split `secret_origination` / `physical_or_broker` / `judgement`, vs `defaulted_to_human` — *a wire exists, or could, and nobody built it*) and an age; `unclassified` is REFUSED. **(d)** `scripts/ci/check_operator_owed.py`, registered UNGATED (a diff-scoped run is blind to the passage of time), measuring carry from the register's OWN git history rather than self-report. **(c) The MHG over-cover was MISCATEGORISED and is the thesis in miniature:** `cancel-ib-order` and `attach-ib-target` had existed all along; it was detect-only because ONE remediation on 2026-08-20 cancelled the leg that MATCHED the journal — which distrusts the SELECTION, not the mechanism. `src/runtime/over_cover_decision.py` makes that selection a pure function with seven non-collapsed states (the `protection_reassert` pattern) and replays the recorded failure's own measured inputs, asserting the journal-matching leg is **KEPT** — plus a test that the choice does not fall out of order id or input order, since `375` happened to be both the higher id and the journal match. **PROVEN — the first item travelled the whole register:** the stray `claude/ib-breaker-peer-close` branch is **deleted**, seeded as a human hand-off by two sessions → re-classified `defaulted_to_human` on MEASURED evidence (a session credential gets **HTTP 403** on `git push --delete` across four attempts while a normal push from the SAME credential in the SAME minute succeeds — a boundary for that credential, not for a runner) → the missing wire built (`prune-merged-claude-branch.yml`) → dispatched → confirmed by an independent `git ls-remote`, not by the workflow's own conclusion. Its dry run **REFUSED first** and printed its evidence, which is the guard working. **Nobody was asked to do anything.** ⚠️ **NOT PROVEN, and it is a defect in this session's own work: the ratchet has fired on a real carry ZERO times.** Two items sat at carry 1/2 and the next register commit should have escalated one; instead `register commits measured` went **3 → 2** because the squash merge collapsed three in-branch register commits into one and reset the carry. The direction is arguably correct (carry means to count SESSIONS; one squashed PR ≈ one session) — the defect is that the two views disagree and **nothing says which is authoritative**, so a session can watch the ratchet approach and never see it fire. > - 2026-08-20 (evening) — **The E2 gate resolved, and E3 answered: the `label_hold` signal is barrier COMPOSITION, the joint hypothesis buys +0.000R, and the one positive cell dies on cost** ([`S-M20-E3-EXIT-MECHANISM-2026-08-20`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M20-E3-EXIT-MECHANISM-2026-08-20.md); `docs/research/e2-gate-decision-and-sol-clustering-2026-08-20.md`, `e3-barrier-geometry-2026-08-20.md`, `e3-joint-lever-screen-2026-08-20.md`). **THE GATE (BL-20260820-E2-NEGATIVE-CONTROL-GATED-POINTWISE-NOT-FWER, resolved).** The rule was committed (`d5251d1`) BEFORE the re-run was dispatched, so the row's "decide before the next run" is provable from history. ⚠️ **The "SOL-panel factor" the entry above reports is WITHDRAWN.** A control BANK pushed through E2's own imported machinery measures the SOL h48 null as CALIBRATED — clear-rate 0.040 vs α 0.05, mean permutation *p* **0.517** (a valid null gives 0.5), and a length-matched comparison null agrees at 0.045. The real cause: `inject_controls` seeds from the run seed and fills in panel row order, so **the noise column is BYTE-IDENTICAL across all three targets of a panel** (verified directly) — the sweep had **8** independent control draws, not 24. Measured co-discard lift on a 400-column bank: **6.8×–14.0×**. Redone under that dependence, **P(≥4 discards) = 0.086, not 0.0298**, and **P(all on one leg | ≥4) = 0.329, not 0.125** — both quoted numbers assumed an independence the runs do not have, and both flip the conclusion. The gate now reads a **rate** over K=64 noise columns (refuse when the Binomial(K,α) tail < 0.01), with `harness_state` in four never-collapsed states incl. **`unchecked`** (K=0: *we did not look*, which is not `valid`). **Measured over 40 seeded sound null panels:** the old single-draw rule discards **2/40 = 5.0%** (α, exactly as predicted), this gate **0/40**. The FWER gate the row itself proposed was designed, measured (2/64 on a narrow family) and **REJECTED** — P(any of K clears) *rises* with K, so it gets more trigger-happy the more carefully you measure it; kept as a no-vote diagnostic. **EDGE vs BARRIER GEOMETRY — settled, and the E3 licence does not survive it.** The horizon arm's limit 2 was the load-bearing one. The terminal barrier alone accounts for **13.9% → 27.0% → 46.6%** of `label_hold`'s entropy across h=12/24/48 **on one leg** (SOL h48 47.4%, agreeing to 0.8pp); P(hold) is 0.017 at the stop, 0.995 at the target, 0.556 at the time stop. **The rung where E2 starts finding hits is the rung where the barrier's share passes ~45%.** And the pooled association **REVERSES inside every stratum** — `dist_to_stop_atr` +0.117 pooled / −0.194 within, `upnl_r` +0.106 / −0.219 — on exactly the features the horizon arm reported clearing FWER, 5 of 27 per h48 panel. The *within* value is stable across legs and rungs; the *pooled* one, which E2 scores, swings with the barrier mix. The stratified view licenses nothing either (`touch` is the barrier the trade LATER reached). **So E3 is NOT licensed by an E2 information score on this label.** **E3, run anyway as a decision-time cost-probed screen — honest negative.** 11 singles + **179 cells**, 503 + 567 trades, 4 anchored strictly-forward folds, all numbers OOS. **The falsifier FAILS on both legs:** XRP's joint grid, given **16.3× the cells, selected the same single cell every fold — +0.000 R**; SOL's arms both lose, so the comparison does not apply. The one positive cell (`bank0.75`, +5.467 R, 4/4 folds) **breaks even at 0.02–0.05 R of extra cost while the repo's own `DEFAULT_FEE_BPS_ROUNDTRIP = 7.5` resolves to 0.082–0.163 R here** — it fails on cost by 2–8×. ⚠️ **A decision-time lever screen is HORIZON-INVARIANT**, verified to full float equality (`5.467111` on both the h12 and h48 panels): the horizon moves only the LABEL, a lever acts on the TRADE, so **no lever inherits the h=48 licence**. **THE RE-RUN MOVED THE HORIZON ARM'S HEADLINE.** All 24 cells re-run under the new rule: **every admissible cell reproduces its published counts EXACTLY** (the gate decides admissibility, not verdicts) — but **the holes swapped legs**. `advantage_r` SOL h24/h48/h96 recover, so **`advantage_r` is now negative on 8 of 8 cells**, a stronger claim than published; `label_hold` **SOL h48 recovers as HIT 3/5/6** (the "hole at the decisive rung" was the gate's own error, not luck); and `label_hold` **XRP h48 + h96 become inadmissible**. ⚠️ **So the flip is carried by SOL ALONE and the horizon arm's "two independent legs" defence is WITHDRAWN for that target.** Measured cause, pooled over 1,536 control draws: **XRP's null is anticonservative at 2.27× α** (87/768, +8.0 sd, *p* ≈ 2×10⁻¹²) while **SOL's is textbook** (38/768, 0.99× α, mean permutation *p* 0.4956) — the original suspicion that SOL was the narrow leg was exactly backwards (`BL-20260820-TRADE-BLOCK-NULL-IS-ANTICONSERVATIVE-ON-XRP`, high). ⚠️ Quote the **leg-level** figure, not the two per-cell firings: one cell re-run under six seeds fires the gate on 2 of 6 — precisely the 0.19 power at K=64 against a 2× null that the rule's own pre-shipped power curve predicted, so the per-cell verdict is a coin flip at that effect size while the leg-level defect replicates across all six. Mechanism NOT established (the cycling distortion is a candidate; the `cv_length` ordering runs the wrong way). **THREE MORE THINGS FILED, and they point somewhere new.** The label's 2.0R barrier does not match the live `tp_at_r: 1.5` and **no harness trade ever realises above +1.5R** (caps at exactly +1.500, 97/503) — E2 has been scoring a counterfactual the fleet cannot execute (`BL-20260820-E2-LABEL-BARRIER-DOES-NOT-MATCH-THE-LIVE-EXIT-POLICY`). The take-profit **LEVEL** has never been a swept dimension in any exit sweep — all 8 lever columns are post-entry overrides, and `m27/ict_scalp_exit_sweep.py` takes `tp_at_r` as a FIXED input and skips any rung `>= tp_at_r` — on a fleet whose own E0 census says a level fixed at entry decides 78.5% of exits, against an MFE distribution of p50 **0.70R** and a 1.5R target reached by **~19%** of trades while half of all trades never use half the 1.0R stop (`BL-20260820-TP-LEVEL-IS-THE-ONE-EXIT-PARAMETER-NEVER-SWEPT`). And the lever break-even sits below the repo's own fee constant, which puts the legs' fee-free means (+0.1376R / +0.1167R) inside their own round-trip cost — filed as **a flag with arithmetic, not a finding** (R is inferred from ATR, the fill side is unestablished), routing the baseline half to `/performance-review` (`BL-20260820-EXIT-LEVER-BREAKEVEN-IS-BELOW-THE-REPO-OWN-FEE-CONSTANT`). **Next is the bracket geometry** (`tp_at_r`, stop distance, `timeout_bars`) through the existing M20 folds and Path A/Path B gate, graded **net of fees** — a new coverage-matrix DIMENSION, not a new gate; any config change is Tier-3. Tier-1 throughout: research tooling + docs + backlog, no `src/`, `config/`, `ml/`, VM or order path, and the trainer VM was not used.
> - 2026-08-20 (late afternoon) — **The E2 negative was HORIZON-BOUND: at 12–24h the panel predicts the SIGN of the hold-vs-exit decision, on two independent legs** ([`S-M20-E2-HORIZON-ARM-2026-08-20`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M20-E2-HORIZON-ARM-2026-08-20.md); `docs/research/e2-horizon-arm-2026-08-20.md`, `.github/workflows/research-e2-horizon-arm.yml`). The entry below recorded E2's `no_feature_beats_control` at a **12-bar (3h) vertical barrier** as a *condition on the answer*, and named the longer-horizon arm as the cheapest follow-up. Run. **Population, on every number:** `ict_scalp` 15m off the Binance public archive 2021-08-16→2026-08-19 — **XRPUSDT 9,761 rows / 503 of 503 trades; SOLUSDT 10,724 rows / 567 of 567** — 27 scored features, xa block `joined` at `row_coverage` **1.0** on all 8 rungs, 4 folds, 1,000 shuffles, α 0.05, `tp_r` 2.0. **Rows, trades and feature count are IDENTICAL across a leg's four rungs**, because the two things that would otherwise move with the horizon were pinned: `expected_hold_bars` (else `bars_in_trade_frac`'s denominator tracks the barrier and the FEATURE SET changes too) and `embargo_bars` (set = horizon, preserving the reference's 12/12 relationship; the splitter already purges on each row's own `label_t1`). Horizons are anchored on a **declared value, not intuited** — the adapter's `timeout_bars: int = 24` with no override — so 12/24/48/96 = 0.5×/1×/2×/4×. ⚠️ **THE VERDICT FLIPS ON `label_hold`:** `no_feature_beats_control` at h=12 and h=24 on both legs → **`informative_features_found` at h=48 (XRP 2/3/5) and h=96 (XRP 3/4/7, SOL 4/5/6)**. Not a max-statistic reshuffle: tracking ONE feature with its own bar beside it (both move — the null widens with the horizon), `feat_dist_to_stop_atr` is monotone in the statistic AND in the **gap** on both legs — XRP −0.0799 → −0.0559 → **+0.0100** → **+0.0657**, SOL −0.0726 → −0.0551 → −0.0067 → **+0.0388** — with fold sign-agreement rising 0.50 → 1.00, and the h=96 hits sharing three features across legs. **`advantage_r` stays negative wherever admissible**, so the horizon buys the decision's SIGN and not its MAGNITUDE. **The 12-bar rung is a REPLICATION CONTROL and it passed on both legs** against a different feed (FWER counts matched the trainer run exactly: XRP 6, SOL 5; verdicts identical on all three targets). ⚠️ **Three limits travel with the result:** every feature that clears is **ENDOGENOUS** — no peer or order-flow column clears at any rung, **so it is the HORIZON that changed the answer, NOT E1's widening**, and §0.2's diagnosis is not rescued; part of the effect may be **barrier geometry** (far from stop ⇒ SL less likely) rather than edge, which E2 cannot distinguish and the M20 net-of-cost gate must; and h=96 is 4× the harness's own timeout, so it asks a strategy-change question wearing an exit question's clothes (h=48 is the defensible rung, and it is the one SOL lost). **Admissibility: 20 of 24.** Four runs returned `harness_invalid` — all on SOL — because the pure-noise negative control cleared the **pointwise** bar while sitting far below the FWER bar the decision uses. E2 gates on the pointwise verdict, which misfires at α≈5% BY CONSTRUCTION; the observed **4/24 = 16.7%** gives P(X≥4 | binom(24,0.05)) = **0.0298**, below the gate's own α, and all four on one leg (P=0.125), pointing at a SOL-panel factor rather than bad luck (`BL-20260820-E2-NEGATIVE-CONTROL-GATED-POINTWISE-NOT-FWER`). **They were NOT re-rolled with a new seed** — a pre-registered gate retried until it passes is not a gate — and the fix must be chosen BEFORE the next run, not after seeing which cells it rescues. The finding survived the hole at SOL h=48 only because h=96 replicated: **luck, not design**, and recorded as such. **Also found:** `feat_bars_in_trade_frac` is rank-identical to `feat_bars_in_trade` **BY CONSTRUCTION** (constant divisor ⇒ rank-preserving ⇒ same feature under Spearman AND under trees), upgrading the entry below's *"identical to 16 s.f."* from coincidence to structure (`BL-20260820-BARS-IN-TRADE-FRAC-RANK-IDENTICAL`); and the Binance archive carries `taker_buy_base`, so **both taker columns are DENSE here** (27 features vs the reference's 25) — the order-flow gap that entry flagged is closed, and order flow clears nothing. **Ran on a free GitHub-hosted-runner lane, NOT the trainer VM** (public feed + backtest = CPU-only; no VM, no lane claim, no contention, $0). **E3 is licensed on `label_hold` at a long horizon** — explicitly not on `advantage_r`, not at 3h. Nothing live touched; no Tier-3 change proposed.
> - 2026-08-20 (afternoon) — **E2 has been RUN for the first time, and its answer depends on which outcome you score — which is itself the finding** ([`S-M20-E2-FEATURE-INFORMATION-2026-08-20`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M20-E2-FEATURE-INFORMATION-2026-08-20.md); `scripts/research/e2_feature_information.py`, `docs/research/e2-feature-information-2026-08-20.md`). E2 is the step the process doc recorded as *"never been run… the reason step E3 has been guesswork"*. **Two preconditions had to be built first and neither existed:** `analyze_exit_head._univariate_fdr` is called on the **POOLED** row set with no folds, purge, embargo or grouping — the *leaky* version of exactly this test, sitting 100 lines below the correct splitter in the same file (`BL-20260820-UNIVARIATE-FDR-IS-POOLED-AND-UNPURGED`) — and **no shuffled-label control existed anywhere in the repo** (`shuffled_label`/`label_shuffle`/`permutation_test` → zero files; the only `shuffle` permutes a FEATURE, not the label). **Population, on every number:** `ict_scalp` XRPUSDT 15m, **10,103 labelled rows from 530/530 trades**, 25 dense columns of which **22 are scoreable**, cross-asset block **`state: joined` at `row_coverage` 1.0** over 175,296 indexed bars — so **the exogenous half E1 shipped was genuinely MEASURED, not unmeasured**. 4 folds, embargo 12, 1,000 shuffles, α 0.05, triple-barrier label at a **12-bar (3h) vertical barrier**, `tp_r` 2.0. Controls valid on all three targets (+ctrl 0.5644 FWER-pass, −ctrl 0.0017 at p 0.880). ⚠️ **The pre-registered primary target says one thing and the decision-relevant target says the opposite.** Against **`forward_r`**: six features clear the family-wise bar, **all endogenous, zero exogenous**. Against **`advantage_r`** (= `forward_r − upnl_r − cost_r`, the incremental value of holding) and **`label_hold`** (its sign): **ZERO features clear, at the family-wise bar AND at the pointwise bar**, where ~1.1 pointwise hits would be expected by chance across 22 features. **Every one of the six collapses by one to two orders of magnitude between the two targets** — `feat_upnl_r` **0.5753 → 0.0062, a factor of 93**; `dist_to_stop_atr` 0.4668 → 0.0168; `dmae_dt` 0.2836 → 0.0092. **This was PREDICTED from reading `src/research/triple_barrier.py` before the scores were interpreted, not discovered by inspecting them:** `forward_r` is measured **FROM ENTRY** (deliberately, so it is comparable to `upnl_r`), so it shares its baseline with every path feature tracking accrued R — a trade up 1.5R now tends to still be up ~1.5R at the time stop, which is arithmetic about where it already **is**, not information about where it is **going**. There is **no lookahead** (feature window ≤ t, label window > t, disjoint by construction), which is exactly what makes it dangerous: a fully controlled, purged, embargoed, correctly FWER-adjusted, **misleading** headline. **REPLICATED on an independent leg**: `ict_scalp` SOLUSDT 15m (**10,786 rows from 580/580 trades**, peers ETHUSDT + XRPUSDT, xa `joined` at coverage 1.0, controls valid) gives the same shape — 5 FWER winners on `forward_r`, all endogenous; **0 FWER and 0 min-p** on both `advantage_r` and `label_hold`; `feat_upnl_r` collapsing 0.5799 → 0.0238 (24×). SOL shows 2 and 1 *pointwise* hits where XRP showed zero, both inside the ~1.1 expected by chance and neither surviving a family-wise rule. ⚠️ **`feat_bars_in_trade` and `feat_bars_in_trade_frac` are ONE feature counted twice** (identical to 16 s.f. — `frac` is a positive scalar multiple and Spearman is invariant), so the family holds **21 distinct tests, not 22**; collapse the duplicate before quoting a family size. **The best exogenous showing across both legs and all six runs is p = 0.068**, short of even the pointwise bar — peer state is the family E1 was built to supply, it is present at full coverage on both legs, and it carries nothing measurable about the increment here. **One hypothesis logged and deliberately NOT promoted:** `bars_in_trade` — the time-in-state term §1.5.2's semi-Markov optimal-stopping result predicts — is the single pointwise whisper (SOL `advantage_r`, p 0.034); it fails both family-wise rules and does not reproduce on XRP, so it is a candidate for a later arm, not a result. **The negative survives a scale-free check that is demonstrably not inert**: null widths differ ~5× by design (wide for trade-autocorrelated path features, tight for near-white peer returns), so a raw max-|stat| bar is conservative for precisely the exogenous block — a Westfall–Young **min-p** companion was added, it **promotes one feature on `forward_r` that the raw rule misses**, and it promotes **nothing** on either decision target. **Consequence for the programme:** §0.2 blamed ~20 failed lever cells on an all-endogenous panel and proposed E1's widening as the fix; E1 delivered it, and E2 measures that **the widened panel does not carry the increment either** — at this leg, this horizon, this substrate — while supplying a mechanism for the earlier failures (levers were built over features that look strongly informative only against a target sharing their baseline). **Disposition is §3.1 — regroup and widen; the thread does NOT close**, and step 1 (*check the substrate before blaming the question*) is already discharged: coverage 1.0, real peers, 530 trades, controls fired. **Next is §3.1 step 4 — CHANGE WHAT IS BEING PREDICTED**, for which E2 is itself the argument (swapping the target moved a top feature 93× and flipped the verdict): survival framings / hazard of adverse excursion, P(give-back beyond X), capital-efficiency-adjusted hold value. ⚠️ **Two scope limits stated rather than discovered later:** `feat_taker_imbalance` + `feat_taker_imbalance_intrade` were **dropped as all-null** (the candle source carries no taker data), so **E2 says nothing about order flow** — a family the literature survey had already flagged as untried; and the 12-bar horizon is a **condition on the answer**, not a property of the fleet. **Also found and filed:** the trainer root fs is back to **42 of 45 GB used** against a row resolved on a *"back under ~80%"* criterion (`BL-20260820-TRAINER-DISK-BACK-TO-94PCT`), and **5 of 6 committed candle files are constant-price placeholders** (300 rows, 1 distinct close each) that nothing at the call site marks as such (`BL-20260820-PLACEHOLDER-CANDLE-FIXTURES-CARRY-NO-MARKER`). Nothing live was touched; no Tier-3 change proposed.

Found only by widening a filter from one strategy to the whole account — every prior read was structurally incapable of seeing it. **THE FIX (Tier-3 + Tier-2, operator-approved in-conversation, PR #9039):** available margin now derives from the USDT **coin** block (`equity − totalPositionIM − totalOrderIM`, all broker-reported) where Bybit actually publishes it; the equity fallback subtracts ESTIMATED pledged margin; and every rejection row is stamped with the basis that produced its size. Validated four ways before shipping — reproduces an independent journal reconstruction to **0.05%**, the venue's own `totalPositionIM` sits **0.22%** from the modelled leverage, it sits inside a contradiction bound derived from Bybit's own refusal, and replayed against the rejection window it sizes `0.011 → 0.008 BTC`, the range the account was ALREADY filling at. **STATUS: DEPLOYED (`5bbb3416`) BUT LIVE-UNVERIFIED** — bybit_2's newest row is 2026-08-11, two days before the deploy, so no row carries a `margin_basis` stamp yet; the backlog row carries a one-read finishing procedure including the outcome that REOPENS it. **THREE OF MY OWN HYPOTHESES WERE REFUTED EN ROUTE** (venue leverage — refuted by the boot log; account-wide scope — refuted by the paper books reading fine; a derivation from `totalEquity − totalInitialMargin` — refuted by reading the VALUES, that field is empty and I had inferred it populated from a key list). **CI caught 8 failures I missed**, two serious: an `UnboundLocalError` on the exact failure path the new stamp exists to record, and a **re-created halt vector** — subtracting pledged margin unconditionally refuses EVERY trade on an account whose journal over-reports open notional (451× measured under netting), caught by a test whose docstring says it must never be edited to accommodate a feature. Root cause of the misses: a `pytest -k` filter that excluded every failing test. **Also shipped:** the AVAX venue-max clamp (filed headline DISPROVED — the leg fills below the cap), the VM-runner sudoers purge on a root-verified post-state, a 14-day test time bomb reddening `main`, and a `pytest-run` short-circuit that let a PR merge having executed no tests. **Deferred, NOT done:** the audit report to `comms/reports/` and the doc-stale backfills.

The mean fell only because the decouple moved `strategy_monitor_loop` into `offloop_hooks`; **the system is not doing less work, it is doing it concurrently.** A mean that improves while the peak worsens is exactly the shape `/api/diag/tick_cost`'s own doc warns to read `max_ms` beside `ticks_measured` for. **The pre-specified fix was the wrong TIER and was NOT shipped:** strategies read `close.iloc[-1]` as the CURRENT PRICE for entry geometry and the monitor reads it for exits, so the TTL bounds the price behind a live order — Tier-3. Shipped instead: `CANDLE_CACHE_TTL_MAX_S` settable at the incumbent **60.0** (byte-for-byte unchanged, one env flip to move) plus a **`fetchby.<consumer>`** cut, because whether a SAFE consumer-scoped TTL exists turns on whether the >= 15m misses are `regime_bar_scoring` (observe-only, dedups to closed bars) or `signal_build` (feeds entry geometry). **Row stays OPEN** — the value is the operator's, with the numbers.

| **M8** | pm-sprint | Strategy tuning | 🔄 IN PROGRESS — kickoff sprint [`S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S0`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S0.md) ships the **canonical parameter-sweep harness** (`scripts/ml/strategy_tune_sweep.py`) that *executes* the M7 gate's `tune_recipe`: ingests the recipe, expands `search_space` (log-uniform / uniform / grid / colon-range), dispatches to the right existing backtester (research-harness `min_confidence` per-value + vwap `threshold` native-sweep seeded in the registry), normalizes heterogeneous net-of-fee metrics, and emits a `strategy_tune_result/v1` packet with an **advisory** Tier-3 value proposal — never writes `config/strategies.yaml`. Canonical doc [`docs/strategy-tuning.md`](docs/strategy-tuning.md); 38 tests. **S1 [`S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S1`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S1.md)** ran the **first real sweep** on the trainer VM (≈5.4 yr BTC 1h) — added a `fixed_args` passthrough (pin live params), robust top-level-JSON extraction (two real-run bugs), and a `--long-only` flag to `backtest_trend.py` for live-parity. First result: `trend_donchian.min_confidence` (live `0.30`) — long-only sweep advises ~`0.60` (net R +25%, expectancy +56%, maxDD −42% vs 0.30), but the optimum sits at the grid boundary and the run is in-sample. **S2 [`S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S2`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S2.md)** added **walk-forward/OOS validation** (`--oos-start`; each value run train+OOS, picks gated on OOS, `train_oos_consistent` flag, in-sample runs flagged not-OOS-validated). Re-ran the trend floor widened `[0.3,0.9]` with an OOS split at 2025-01-01 — **key finding: the live `0.30` is net-NEGATIVE out-of-sample** (−14 R / −0.111 exp over 2025-26; the S1 +66 R was in-sample, dominated by 2021-24). OOS turns positive only at **≥0.55** (and ~thirds drawdown); plateau 0.55–0.90 positive, harness picks 0.90 (train/OOS-consistent). 44 tests. **S3 [`S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S3`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M8-STRATEGY-TUNING-S3.md)** added **k-fold anchored walk-forward** (`--wf-folds`; OOS aggregated across folds + a `robust`=positive-in-every-fold gate), expanded the registry to trend's structural params (`trail_mult`/`atr_stop_mult`/`donchian`/`atr_period`), and shipped `GET /api/bot/strategies/{name}/tune`. **3-fold result (2021–2026): the live `0.30` floor is net-positive in only 2/3 folds; 3/3 robustness begins at `0.60`** (OOS expectancy ~2×, drawdown −40%), uniformly robust through 0.90. Floor raise now OOS+3-fold validated → **draft Tier-3 PR proposes `0.60`** (operator-gated). 53 tests. Next: dashboard panel consuming `/tune` (preview-branch flow); registry-driven structural-param sweeps. **APPLIED 2026-06-10:** operator approved → `trend_donchian.min_confidence` **0.30 → 0.60 merged (#3181) + deployed** to the live trader; dashboard tune-results panel shipped (ict-trader-dashboard #87). M8 tooling (S0–S3) complete + first tune applied to live. |

Bot: **FCM data messages now ride `android.priority=HIGH`** (were default/NORMAL → Doze batched them → operator's "notifications arrive in delayed bursts"); **liveness-watchdog `--boot-grace-seconds 600`** suppresses heartbeat missing/stale alerts (and autoheal) for 10 min after a host boot and emits no "recovered" ping, so a VM reboot stops spamming `[CRITICAL] heartbeat stale` + `[OK] recovered` — a still-stale heartbeat past the window alerts as a genuine failure-to-recover. | **M14** | auto-claude (ML-optimization track) | **ML Optimization Program** — multi-session program to lift model quality: validation discipline → break the decision-model data wall → better features → regime plumbing → MLOps. Framing finding (deep-research + codebase inventory, 2026-06-03): on intraday tabular features, architecture is rarely the binding constraint — labeling/validation/sample-size/features are. | 🔄 IN PROGRESS — **Phase 0 (S0–S4 validation & training discipline) + Phase 1 (S5–S8 break the decision-model data wall) COMPLETE 2026-06-03**; **Phase 2 (S9–S12 better features) SHIPPED 2026-06-04**; **Phase 3 (S13–S15b regime plumbing) + Phase 4 (S16–S18 MLOps) landed/in-review** — S13 per-bar scoring ✅ MERGED (PR #2778); S14 HMM, S15/S15a/S15b regime-router, S16 ADWIN drift-retrain, S17 train/serve parity, S18 promotion-readiness all shipped to disk (trainer-side tooling + research_only manifests; several behind default-disabled trainer timers / draft PRs) — see the per-sprint status in § "M14 — ML Optimization Program" below (do not read this summary as "S13–S18 not started"). **Phase 0:** S1 (#2674) purged & embargoed WF-CV; S2 (#2676/#2679) recency `sample_weight`; S3 (#2682) Optuna HPO over the purged folds; **S4 (#2685) promotion gates that COMPUTE PASS/FAIL — `gate-check` is a mechanical champion-challenger packet (OOS edge vs baseline under purged WF-CV + KS/PSI drift bounds + shadow volume/soak), enforcement stays Tier-3.** **Phase 1:** S5 (#2689) triple-barrier `setup_candidates` (15.7k BTC / 6.7k MES dense candidates); S6 (#2693) meta-labeling model + leak-free `live_holdout` real-trade eval; S7 (#2698) backtest-augmented labels (`source`/`include_backtest` + recorder); S8 (#2701) cross-symbol joint BTC+MES build. **Two honest findings the discipline surfaced:** the meta-label does NOT yet beat the majority baseline on real BTC trades (S6), and MES has 0 real trades so transfer is unmeasurable (S8) — both point to the same root (too little realistic labeled data), now attackable via the shipped machinery. Net: model-quality is measured leakage-free end-to-end, "is it ready to promote?" is non-discretionary, and the data wall has the tooling to be broken. **Next: Phase 2 (S9 range-based vol estimators, Tier-1) or S13 (3.1) per-bar regime scoring (Tier-2, live-runtime — highest-leverage regime unblock); the S6 follow-up (`MB-20260603-002`, signal-log event source — S-MLOPT-S6-FU, PR #2712) shipped + evaluated 2026-06-03: **honest negative** (signal-log acc 0.526 < CUSUM 0.670 < 0.756 baseline) which isolated the synthetic *label* (not the event sampler) as the gap → `MB-20260603-002` RESOLVED-NEGATIVE. Next lever shipped same day: **S-MLOPT-S6-FU-2** (`MB-20260603-003`, backtest-labeled `setup_candidates` event source — real-execution harness outcomes as the train distribution; Tier-1 enabler + bridge + Tier-3 manifest at `research_only`); its trainer-VM eval landed (#2718/#2724): across all FOUR label sources (CUSUM 0.670 / signal-log 0.526 / backtest-1h 0.756 / backtest-native 0.741) backtest labels are the best by precision (0.40-0.50 > 0.244 base rate) but NONE beats the n=352 majority baseline — a confirmed **data-scale floor** (`MB-20260603-003` resolved). **The feature lever moved the needle where labels didn't — Phase 2 ("better features") ran S9–S12:** **S9** range-based vol — A/B positive on BTC (purged-CV confirmed leak-free), **promoted to shadow** on all three BTC regime heads 2026-06-04; MES range-vol was mixed → held. **S11** crypto funding/OI features (#2739) — full infra shipped, **honest negative** (Bybit OI history retention-limited to ~8 days; funding z/extremes carried). **S10** order-flow / VPIN (#2751) — estimator core + forward-capture side-car **deployed + verified live on the trainer** (`market_microstructure` accruing ~288 bars/day; A/B is weeks out by nature, `MB-20260604-002`). **S12** cross-asset/macro for MES (Part A, S-MLOPT-S12) — VIX/term-structure/DXY/rates conditioning into `market_features` v6 (one-day-lagged, leakage-safe) + research_only `mes-regime-5m-lgbm-macro-v1`; A/B pending the next MES v6 rebuild (`MB-20260604-004`); the `account_context` half deferred (`MB-20260604-003`, needs as-of-signal snapshots). **Next: S13 (3.1) per-bar regime scoring** (Tier-2 live-runtime — the highest-leverage regime unblock), or drain the pending feature A/Bs (S10/S12) as data accrues. The "MES-no-trades operational issue" was root-caused as the strategy correctly waiting for a Donchian-30 breakout (no bug; tracked in health backlog `BL-20260603-001`).** Full plan + per-session detail: [`docs/ml/optimization-roadmap.md`](docs/ml/optimization-roadmap.md); sprint breakdown in § "M14 — ML Optimization Program" below. Trainer-side tooling is Tier-1 autonomous; new manifests + live-runtime/order-path changes stay Tier-2/3 (operator-gated). |
| **M13** | auto-claude (separate session — AI Analyst track) | AI Analyst — server-side LLM agent over live trading data, surfaced as a new FastAPI router on `:8001` | ✅ S1 + S2 COMPLETE 2026-05-26 — provider-flexible analyst now live on the trader VM. **NOT a registry model.** Tier-1 read-only; no influence on the order path. Three switchable modes via `INSIGHTS_MODEL_MODE`: `template` (rule-based, $0, deterministic, **active default 2026-05-26**), `anthropic` (Claude API, requires credit), `gemini` (Google Generative Language API, paid-tier required to lift `limit:0` free-tier quotas). Four cached endpoints under `/api/bot/insights/*` (`summary`, `recent`, `strategy/{name}`, `health`), each returning `{summary_md, grade, signals[], data_window, row_counts, generated_at, cache_age_seconds, model_id, cache_present, cache_path}`. Two-tier cadence: **fast tier** (`ict-insights-generator.timer`, every 15 min) drives the 3 globals; **slow tier** (`ict-insights-generator-strategies.timer`, every 60 min) drives the 6 per-strategy narratives. Two persistent tables: `insights_history` (every run, decoded JSON) + `insights_usage` (per-call tokens + estimated cost + budget gate). Read endpoints `/api/bot/insights/{history,usage}` surface those to the Streamlit dashboard's Insights tab + Overview card + usage panel + per-endpoint history expanders. Three Tier-1 system-actions: `enable-insights-generator`, `disable-insights-generator`, `inspect-insights` (full state snapshot), `kick-insights` (manual cycle fire). Verification gate **met:** live VM ran a 21:03 UTC cycle that produced grounded prose against real trades (vwap concern grade @ 5% win rate over 20 closed trades; trend_donchian watch grade; overall summary at +$1.34/24h, 50% win rate). See header addendum above + [`S-ALPACA-PIPELINE-AUDIT-2026-07-07`](docs/sprint-logs/S-ALPACA-PIPELINE-AUDIT-2026-07-07.md). **`alpaca_live` sub-$100 proxy promotion 2026-07-07 (S-PROXY-EQUITIES-ALPACA-LIVE, Tier-3, operator-approved):** added the two real-money proxy cells `splg_trend_long_1d` (SPLG ~$84, S&P proxy for SPY) + `iaum_pullback_1d` (IAUM ~$41, gold proxy for GLD) — the pair that clears BOTH the compat survival gate AND ≥1-whole-share affordability at ~$150 equity; risk caps **normalized back to 5% max-DD / 5% daily-loss at `risk_pct 0.02`** (superseding the 2026-06-30 10% test-account escalation that had blown the survival gate). SCHA not promoted (marginal, paper-only). Real-money roster on `alpaca_live` = **16 ETF cells**. ⚠️ **ALPACA SHORT-SIDE IS BLOCKED ON TWO OPEN ROWS, AND THE ANSWER IS NOT 'ENABLE SHORTING' (operator directive, reaffirmed 2026-08-25: *"we're not turning on short... find the proxy symbols"*; originally given 2026-08-23).** A session that reports enabling broker-side shorting as the path is contradicting a standing directive — that happened on 2026-08-25, which is why this is recorded on the milestone and not only in a backlog row. The account is **not fundable until both clear**. **(1) Scope** — `side_filter` is per-STRATEGY and every `both` leg `alpaca_live` routes is also routed to the paper soak book, the portfolio mirror and `ib_paper`, so long-only there suppresses shorts everywhere; **✅ ALREADY BUILT AND LIVE — shipped by #10206 (`ad70907`), NOT outstanding.** `src/runtime/account_side_filter.py` + the fold into `coordinator.multi_account_execute`'s `effective_dry` + tests + `alpaca_live: side_filter: long` in `accounts.yaml`, all on `main`. `alpaca_paper` / `alpaca_portfolio` / `ib_paper` keep trading BOTH sides, so the short-side soak that grades the decision still accrues, and a suppressed short is still JOURNALLED as a would-be trade so *"what did long-only cost or save?"* stays measurable. ⚠️ **This entry previously said the gate still had to be built — it was written from a backlog row left `open` after the code shipped, and repeated to the operator twice on 2026-08-25. Field beats comment: check the code before reporting a blocker from a row.** `BL-20260823-ALPACA-LONG-ONLY-CANNOT-BE-SCOPED-TO-ONE-ACCOUNT` is RESOLVED by #10206. **(2) Proxies** — zero inverse instruments are declared. Tickers were verified against sources for the first time on 2026-08-25 and the book SPLITS: **buildable** SPY→**SH** (−1x, ER 0.89%, ~$1B), QQQ→**PSQ** (−1x, 0.95%, $759M), TLT→**TBF** (−1x, 0.95%, $80M), IEF→**TBX** (−1x, 0.95%, **$14M — thin**); **no usable product** for GLD/IAUM/SLV/GDX, where only −2x daily funds (GLL, ZSL, DUST), −3x ETNs (GDXD) or a zombie ETN (DGZ, *"minimal assets and negligible daily trading"*) exist — and that is precisely where the short flow concentrates (`gld_pullback_1d` 8/8, `gdx_pullback_1d` 7/7, `iaum_pullback_1d` 6/6, `slv_pullback_1d` 5/5, `gld_pullback_1h` 38/51). **Operator decision 2026-08-25: the four metals legs are recorded LONG-ONLY WITH REDUCED FLOW and their expected contribution restated** — closing that half by decision, since there is no product to find; and **SPY→SH + QQQ→PSQ are built FIRST** to prove the pattern end-to-end before the thin ones. ⚠️ Every proxy is **daily-rebalanced**, so even at −1x it drifts over the multi-day holds these legs run — a close substitute, never a short — and the 0.89–0.95% expense ratio must sit INSIDE the backtest. `BL-20260823-NO-INVERSE-ETF-INSTRUMENTS-DECLARED`. **GO-LIVE DECIDED 2026-08-29 (operator, in conversation) — the five open questions are now four answers plus a sequence; do NOT re-litigate them.** (1) **CASH, permanently** (`capacity.multiplier == 1` measured) — so no shorting, a gross-exposure multiple can never exceed 1.0x, and PDT is inert. (2) plumbing-test leg **`tlt_pullback_1h`** (TLT ~$83 so 2 shares fit; n=171, the best-evidenced leg on the roster). (3) per-trade notional cap **100%, i.e. NO cap beyond the cash account's own 1.0x limit**. (4) **`risk_pct` stays 0.05**. ⚠️ (3)+(4) are coherent rather than reckless AT THIS FUNDING and the reason must travel with them: population = the 12 `alpaca_live` journal rows in 2026-08-20 -> 2026-08-29 whose sizing SUCCEEDED (TLT 9, SLV 3), every one at **qty 2.0** — the sizer demands ~26.7 TLT shares (~11x the account) and the **CASH BALANCE clamps it to 2**, so realised risk at the declared stop is **~$0.75**, not the $10 that 5% implies. Cash is the binding cap, not `risk_pct`; the clamp weakens if the account is ever funded up. **Sequence: read the settlement soak -> Tier-2 flip `ALPACA_CASH_SETTLEMENT_MODE` to `apply` (allowlisted to `alpaca_live` only) -> route the leg (Tier-3, the live-trading moment).** **T+1 SETTLEMENT NOW MODELLED** ([#10408](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10408), `src/runtime/cash_settlement.py`, merged + deployed, shipped at **`annotate`** so it binds nothing): `basis = min(venue_buying_power, venue_cash - our_unsettled)`, correct under all four combinations of (Alpaca nets unsettled / does not) x (we saw the sale / did not), because the distinguishing state has never existed on this account and both paper mirrors are MARGIN books that cannot exhibit it. Trading days come from the venue's own `/v2/calendar` — counting T+1 with `market_hours.py`, which models no holidays, would credit proceeds a day EARLY across one and manufacture the violation. ⚠️ **`would_have_reduced_usd` reads 0.00 on `alpaca_live` however long anyone waits** — `recent_sales` needs `status='closed'` with a non-null exit price in a 10-day window and that account has NONE; T+1 binds only after a sale, so an account that has never traded cannot demonstrate it. Circular by construction, not a gap; the mechanism evidence is on the PAPER accounts, which do sell. A weekday repo timer now carries the precondition ([#10412](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10412), `alpaca-settlement-soak-watch.yml`) and grades the soak against the newest alpaca dispatch FIRST, so an empty soak is never collapsed. **THE $200 SIZING WALL IS NOW CONFIRMED ON THE LIVE ACCOUNT, not derived** ([#10411](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10411)): population = the 400 most recent `trades` rows, 2026-08-20T08:12Z -> 2026-08-29T14:04Z, of which **23 are `alpaca_live` and all 23 are `rejected`**; five carry `sized_qty=0 with balance=200.10` and they are exactly GLD/SPY/QQQ — the legs' own ATR stops, real balance. **IWM produced no row and stays UNCONFIRMED.** Full plan: [`docs/claude/WORKPLAN-2026-08-29.md`](docs/claude/WORKPLAN-2026-08-29.md) Lane A; sprint log [`S-ALPACA-T1-AND-WORKPLAN-2026-08-29`](docs/sprint-logs/S-ALPACA-T1-AND-WORKPLAN-2026-08-29.md). |
| **M16** | pm-sprint (multi-session, ML-risk track) | **Unified Confidence Risk Architecture** — replace the thicket of independent gates (regime hard-gate, advisory/news downsize, stage gate) with a small set of composite confidence "lenses" that collectively ADVISE sizing + trade-selection (the only hard refusal is a per-lens no-trade floor); risk stays per-strategy-per-trade, sized by confidence × per-trade risk × available margin, no per-account max-position cap. Design: [`docs/unified-confidence-risk-DESIGN.md`](docs/unified-confidence-risk-DESIGN.md). | 🔄 IN PROGRESS — research proposal (2026-06-15) now shipping phased + observe-first: **Design-A regime-router ML vol-verdict LIVE for BTC** (`REGIME_ML_VERDICT_MODE=use`, per-SYMBOL advisory head, 2026-06-28); **Design-B conviction-driven sizing** — the *reductive* apply path is now **LIVE on the demo account** (`CONVICTION_SIZING_MODE=apply` / `DIRECTION=reductive` / `ACCOUNTS=bybit_1`, operator-approved 2026-08-05; symmetric c_strat-only sizing had FAILED the earlier A/B at 4.5× worse maxDD so it stays off — `CONVICTION_SIZING_MODE`, PR #4748). **C1 deployable-evidence A/B (365d, per-symbol, budget-matched, net-of-cost) RAN 2026-08-05 → PARTIAL** ([`C1-conviction-sizing-evidence-2026-08-04.md`](docs/research/C1-conviction-sizing-evidence-2026-08-04.md), via the `c1-conviction-ab` free-runner workflow): reductive cuts maxDD on BTC (Δ −$1,810, n=477) but WORSENS it on SOL (Δ +$1,786, n=617), ETH below-floor (n=12), all over a **net-negative population** — NOT a clean cross-symbol win. **Continuation (Tier-3, operator-gated):** the demo flip STAYS demo-only; widening reductive conviction to any real-money account needs a clean cross-symbol maxDD win at solid n on a floor-clearing (ideally profitable) population — re-run the `c1-conviction-ab` A/B once the underlying books turn net-positive (the losing-population caveat means the conviction *shape* can't be graded as a profitability edge today), and meanwhile observe the live demo soak. Label-augmentation backfill that feeds this class is now durable + verified (weekly `research-backtest-augment` → trainer land + nightly merge). The conviction (`conviction_sizing` / `conviction_arbitration`) + M18 allocator soaks continue accruing observe-only. Every order-influencing step is backtest-A/B-gated + operator-approved (Tier-3). ⚠️ **PREMISE MEASURED 2026-08-26 AND IT FAILS THE JOB M18 ASSIGNED IT.** The `confidence` scalar this milestone is built on measures **OOS AUC 0.522 against a pre-registered 0.55 gate** (M18 expanding walk-forward, n_eval=1,236); every sibling input sits 0.47–0.53. ⚠️ Do NOT over-read it: that is a verdict on **cross-candidate SELECTION** on that candidate set, NOT a finding that confidence is useless for sizing, gating, or within-strategy use. Per [`WORKPLAN-2026-08-26`](docs/claude/WORKPLAN-2026-08-26.md) Lane D1, re-scope this milestone to a use confidence CAN serve, or park it explicitly — a silent continuation is the disposition being ruled out. |
| **M18** | pm-sprint (multi-session) | **Portfolio Capital Allocator (AI)** — a portfolio-level decision layer that sees the full set of candidate trades from ALL strategies/symbols/accounts each tick and allocates the limited capital/risk budget to the cost-aware EV-optimal subset (fee/funding-aware, correlation-aware), so money is never stranded on a worse trade when a better one exists. The N-way / portfolio companion to the three single-decision optimization units (A pairwise-flip-EV, B confidence-sizing, C prop-exit-banking, merged #4994). **Key scoping finding:** the centralized-allocator *seam already exists* (M11 `AllocatorInterface`/`PassthroughAllocator` behind `CENTRALIZED_ALLOCATOR`, fed one signal at a time, no EV/caps) — this is a smarter allocator impl fed the full candidate batch, not a new layer. Design: [`docs/research/capital-allocation-ai-DESIGN.md`](docs/research/capital-allocation-ai-DESIGN.md). | 📋 PROPOSED 2026-06-29 — Tier-3, **PROPOSE-ONLY** (no live src/config changed). Phased shadow-first plan in § "M18 — Portfolio Capital Allocator" below; ml-review-backlog items `MB-20260629-ALLOC-RANKER` / `-COSTCAP` / `-CORR` opened for the model/experiment proposals. Nothing graduates without per-trade cost capture (data gap), shadow-soak regret evidence, a backtest A/B, and operator approval. ⚠️ **2026-08-30 — THE SOAK'S REGRET IS MOSTLY NOT AN ALLOCATION-QUALITY PROBLEM, AND THIS RE-BASES M18'S OWN EVIDENCE.** Measured over the whole `allocator_soak` (n=277, `/api/bot/allocator/soak`): **113 of the 137 disagreements (82.5%) are the SAME STRATEGY routed to TWO ACCOUNTS colliding in `aggregate_intents`**, which picks ONE winner per SYMBOL *globally, before account fan-out* — ETH donchian 56, SOL donchian 34, ETH pullback 23, against only **24** genuine cross-strategy contests. Twin rows carry HIGHER mean regret (0.7664) than the fleet (0.694923), so the headline `mean_regret` is INFLATED by an artifact a smarter allocator cannot fix — **fanning out per account removes that 82.5% by construction, with no ranker involved.** Any future read of this soak must separate the two populations before grading allocator quality; the 2026-06-30 finding that EV-scorer SELECTION does not beat dumb priority was measured on the un-separated set. ⚠️ The arbitration is account-**BLIND**, not prop-biased: the prop leg wins both donchian pairs and **loses** ETH pullback — so this is not a funding-class rule and must not be described as one. Live consequence, and the reason it is not academic: `trend_donchian_sol` has emitted **144 actionable buy signals since 08-01 and written ZERO journal rows on `bybit_1`**, its last row of any kind being 2026-06-29 (`BL-20260830-TREND-DONCHIAN-SOL-SIGNALS-144-TIMES-AND-JOURNALS-NOTHING-ON-BYBIT-1`, and the same defect Lane R's **R2** recorded from the backtest side). The remedy — fan out arbitration per `(account, symbol)` — is **Lane P/P3, Tier-3**; the operator chose that direction 2026-08-30, and it ships behind a `*_MODE` gate defaulting to `annotate` with an accounts allowlist whose EMPTY value means NONE. Evidence + method: [`S-LANE-P-SIGNAL-JOURNAL-AXIS-2026-08-30`](docs/sprint-logs/S-LANE-P-SIGNAL-JOURNAL-AXIS-2026-08-30.md). |
| **M19** | pm-sprint (multi-session, AI-model track) | **AI Model Strategy — new model *types*** — the successor to the M14 optimization program. Every model today is a narrow tabular classifier over hand-engineered features; M19 opens the unexplored frontier of new model *kinds*: representation/embedding models, unsupervised regime discovery, deep sequence models, a shared **"reads-everything" self-supervised encoder** (the operator's centerpiece — fed a wide multi-asset panel *beyond* what we trade), cross-sectional rankers, and RL sizing/exit policies. **Key reframe:** the binding constraint is **labels, not compute** → self-supervised representation learning (label-free) is the flagship, and deep models enter as *encoders feeding the existing LightGBM heads*, not end-to-end replacements. Phased across compute tiers ($0 free-CPU → ~$10/mo gated spot-GPU bursts → larger long-term). Design: [`docs/research/ai-model-strategy-roadmap-2026-07-01.md`](docs/research/ai-model-strategy-roadmap-2026-07-01.md). | 📋 PROPOSED 2026-07-01 — **PROPOSE-ONLY** (no live src/config/ml changed). Phased shadow-first plan in § "M19 — AI Model Strategy" below; every type graduates observe-only through `candidate → shadow → advisory`, order-influence steps are backtest-A/B-gated + operator-approved. Tier-0 (free CPU) work can start now; the ~$10/mo paid-compute tier is spec'd but spending stays gated behind an explicit operator go-signal. |

Recorded as MEASURABILITY (`exit_head_replayable` / `exit_head_deferred_to_replay`), **not** a `fidelity` upgrade — the harness never applies the head, so flipping fidelity would overclaim on the field the research→results gate reads. **Tier-3 open:** the live `trail_decay_arm_r: 6.49` was fitted on the non-live-faithful trail — reductive lever, so a tuning-basis defect not an incident; the M20 params want a re-sweep on the converged engine (`BL-20260808-TRAIL-LEVER-TUNED-ON-NON-LIVE-FAITHFUL-TRAIL`). **ENGINE RETIRED 2026-08-09** ([`S-M20-TREND-ENGINE-RETIREMENT-2026-08-09`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M20-TREND-ENGINE-RETIREMENT-2026-08-09.md), PR #8660): the second engine is now a hard-fail shim behind a `trend-engine-convergence-guard` that detects a retired copy by the ABSENCE of an engine entry point (AST-read, never prose) and asserts its own denominator. 5 coupled tests decided PER FILE on measurement — 4 engine-independent (repointed, no number moved), 1 genuinely engine-specific (ported with re-derived numbers; the trade SET differs, not just the values). `_load`/`_resample` lifted verbatim to `scripts/candle_io.py` (NOT repointed at the engine's own reader, which cannot read the IBKR JSONL — `BL-20260809-TWO-CANDLE-READERS-DIVERGE-ON-JSONL`). `regime_matrix` re-verified across the swap: +1.5727 net R/trending → −0.2246/transitional on the smoke corpus (n=1, direction only) — **any regime-matrix number from before 2026-08-09 was produced on the wrong engine**. Exit-head artifacts now record `train_start`/`train_end` and the replay emits `in_sample_bars`/`forward_bars`/`forward_trades` + a forward-only delta, so a replay states its own in-sample fraction as a FIELD (`BL-20260808-EXIT-HEAD-MANIFEST-RECORDS-NO-TRAINING-WINDOW` closed). **Tier-3 OPEN — the trail re-sweep answered, and the live values FAIL** ([`M20-trail-decay-resweep-2026-08-09`](docs/research/M20-trail-decay-resweep-2026-08-09.md); BTCUSDT 1h 2023-01-01→2026-07-22, 250 trades / 175 IS / 75 OOS, config-exact, 15 cells): the live `arm6.49/tight2.5` improves net_R in both windows but WORSENS IS maxDD (+0.4220R), so the P4.4 'improves both axes' claim does not reproduce on the live-faithful engine; `tight2.0` strictly dominates it; and the lever is provably INERT in 2026-to-date (max peak-R 4.593 < arm 6.49 over 35 trades). Leg is OOS-negative either way — a tuning-basis correction, not a profitability fix. **DECIDED 2026-08-09 — HOLD at `tight_mult: 2.5`; the retune was approved CONDITIONALLY, tested, and WITHDRAWN.** The operator approved 2.0 conditional on testing it first; per-trade attribution ([`m20_trail_attribution.py`](scripts/research/m20_trail_attribution.py), trainer-diag #8672/#8676/#8677) then showed the proposal is **worse where the lever acts**: only **17 of 250** trades arm it (3 of 75 OOS), and across those 17 tight-2.0 loses **1.8395R** — it gains exactly +0.2R on 14 (the mechanical `(2.5−2.0)×ATR ÷ (2.5×ATR)` when both arms stop on the same bar) and loses 4.6395R on 3 where the tighter stop fires earlier. Its apparent +0.2960R aggregate edge is a **sequencing artifact**: +2.1355R of it comes from 3 trades present in only one book after one shifted exit moved `next_idx` over a week in June 2023. Negative at every arm with a usable sample (3.0/4.0/5.0/6.49); only 8.0 is positive and `TOO_THIN` (n=7). Option B (remove) is also unsupported — vs OFF the lever is **+9.53R at identical full-tape maxDD**. **A live soak could not have tested this**: the arm is 6.49R and 2026's max peak-R is 4.593, so `exit_lever_soak` would accrue ZERO rows and read *clean*. `config/strategies.yaml` UNCHANGED (verified in `main`); `BL-20260808-TRAIL-LEVER-TUNED-ON-NON-LIVE-FAITHFUL-TRAIL` closed `resolved_hold`. The coverage cell is now **`shipped_gate_failed`** (#8687) — a new status, because `honest_negative` implies not-live and `shipped` asserts a validation that no longer reproduces. **Coverage matrix restructured (#8693):** bundled multi-leg rows exploded to ONE ROW PER LEG (23 → 50 rows, 184 → 400 cells) — a bundled row carried one status for a whole family, so it described only the leg that passed. Honest live-leg figure: **304/376 = 80.9% across 47 live legs**; `shipped` share fell 9.2% → 6.0% while *resolved overall rose* 76.6% → 79.5% (most bundled cells were legitimately negative for every leg). Un-run cells (`ada`/`avax` 4h `trail_decay`, recorded as negatives "consistent with the family's fails") are now `pending` — an un-run cell is not a negative. **`exit_ladder` on `ict_scalp` is DONE 2026-08-09/10** ([`S-M20-LADDER-AND-COLLAPSED-STATES-2026-08-09`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M20-LADDER-AND-COLLAPSED-STATES-2026-08-09.md) + [`S-M20-LADDER-VERDICTS-AND-PAIRS-CLOSE-PATH-2026-08-10`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M20-LADDER-VERDICTS-AND-PAIRS-CLOSE-PATH-2026-08-10.md), PR #8712): the harness gained the ladder lever and **7 of 8 legs were swept config-exact** (mgc stays `blocked:data_missing` — its XAU proxy frame is gone from the trainer). Population 28 cells: **26 `honest_negative`, 2 PASSED** — `ict_scalp_sol_15m` `bank0.25@1R` + `bank0.5@1R`, clearing IS+OOS on net_R AND maxDD then surviving the yearly walk-forward 3/4. All 28 cells were **independently reproduced** on a second run. Live coverage **312 → 319 / 376**. **The fleet-wide prior that 'banking loses net_R everywhere' does NOT generalize and the memo's § 11.1 a-priori argument for it is RETRACTED** (memo § 10.6): over the 28 cells net_R *rose* in 6/28 IS and 8/28 OOS, maxDD *rose* (got worse) in 14/28 OOS, and 2 cells passed — so 'P(pass)=0 a priori' is refuted by counterexample. The prior is real for the trend/pullback families (fat right tail) and does not transfer to a 1.5R-capped scalp. Shipping the SOL cells is **operator-approved but NOT built**: it is not a config edit — the materialized ExitPlan is journalled for soak and nothing reads it back to drive an order (M20 P4). **Capital efficiency promoted to a SHIPPING criterion 2026-08-10** (operator-approved Tier-3): the `exit-refinement` skill's P2 gate gains **Path B** (`net_r_per_capital_day` improves IS+OOS AND maxDD not worse AND net_R falls no more than a floor), which still passes the SAME walk-forward — a second door, never a lower bar. Its thresholds are deliberately **UNSET** pending the first sweep's distribution. Metric single-homed in `scripts/capital_efficiency.py`. Motivating live measurement: **24 of 25 open positions have NO time/stall exit lever**, incl. a real-money `eth_pullback_2h` held **149 bars** on a 2h strategy at −0.33R (`BL-20260810-NO-STALL-EXIT-CAPITAL-SITS-IN-DEAD-TRADES`). Remaining M20 gaps: **`exit_head_ml`** (31 of the 57 pending live cells — the single largest open block) and the pullback-family stale/giveback re-sweep that sets Path B's thresholds. **PATH B'S THRESHOLD QUESTION IS ANSWERED 2026-08-10 — AND THE ANSWER IS "SET NONE"** ([`M20-path-b-floor-and-trail-widen-2026-08-10`](docs/research/M20-path-b-floor-and-trail-widen-2026-08-10.md), PR #8756; the sentence immediately above expected the re-sweep to SET a threshold, which is why this correction sits here rather than in a new row). The fleet sweep ran to **575 cells across 43 legs** (`docs/research/m20-sweep-corpus.jsonl`, live-parity capped-TP geometry, split `2025-07-01`) and **both** candidate predictors returned **`no_separation`** — *we looked and found nothing*, distinct from the `insufficient_population` the 10-leg census gave: a base-rate **floor** (52 walk-forwarded cells / 22 legs, 15 floors, best p=0.608 vs a Bonferroni bar of 0.00333) and a `dN/N_b` **cap** (42 thresholds, best p=0.421). At **14 of the 15 floors the arm a floor would REJECT generalises BETTER than the arm it would admit**, so a floor set anywhere on this evidence removes the better-generalising cells. **Two claims from the 10-leg census are FALSIFIED and must not be re-quoted:** "zero gradeable legs sit below rate 1.0" (**6 of 36 do**; lowest `gdx_pullback_1d` at **0.454**, essentially the 0.40 that motivated the floor) and "the trail-widen is a real in-sample effect, 7 of 8, p=0.035" (at n=42 it is **25/42, p=0.14** — not significant; OOS 16/42). The exposure that IS real is not the weak-book case the floor targeted but the SIZE OF THE GRANT: `allowed = D_b × (dN/N_b)` gives **4 of 18 Path B rows more than half the base book's entire drawdown**, and `tlt_pullback_1h trail4` **170%** of it. That is a risk-appetite call, not a statistical one; the only non-arbitrary bound is the structural **`dN/N_b ≤ 1.0`** — where a share becomes an expansion. **APPROVED + IMPLEMENTED 2026-08-11** (Tier-3, operator-approved in chat) in `drawdown_exchange_rate`: the entitlement is clamped at `D_b`, the cap enters `passes` (not only the printed allowance), a cap refusal carries `reason: "grant_exceeds_base_drawdown"` distinct from a rate refusal, and `allowed_d_max_dd_uncapped` is still reported so a reader sees what was clamped. ⚠️ **READ IT CORRECTLY — three misreadings, documented in `.claude/skills/exit-refinement/SKILL.md`:** it caps the ENTITLEMENT and never the ASK (`grant_capped: true` ≠ "too risky"; most capped rows *improve* drawdown — `tlt_pullback_1h trail4` has ratio 1.70 and asks **−0.69R**); it changes **ZERO verdicts** on the measured population and that is not a defect but the point — it is **prophylactic**, re-graded against the corpus at **5 rows capped, 0 verdicts changed**, because none of the 31 over-entitled rows actually asks more than `D_b` (largest real ask +0.78R vs a 15.35R base); and `grant_ratio > 1.0` is not a failing row, `passes` is. **The claim that it "binds 1 of 18 rows" was WRONG and was caught by measuring before shipping** — a risk control that controls nothing, described as one that does, is worse than none. **It is a THIRD threshold and sets neither of Path B's original two** ("improves materially", "the net_R floor"), which remain deliberately unset. Nothing from the sweep is recommended for promotion: 21 Path A PASSes but **9 sit on an OOS base under 20 trades** (`spy_trend_long_1d` ×4 on **three**; `beats()` has no minimum-n — `BL-20260810-PATHA-GATE-HAS-NO-MINIMUM-N`), and the only PASS on a real denominator is on a retired `enabled: false` leg. Reporting fix shipped in the same PR: `path_b_wf_pass` never meant the drawdown-rate gate passed and **6 of 18 rows fail it**, so the verdict now carries a three-state `path_b_rate_ok` plus a `grant%` column. **Separately, the 60s exit-evaluation ask is now measured** ([`M20-exit-monitor-decouple-evidence-2026-08-10`](docs/research/M20-exit-monitor-decouple-evidence-2026-08-10.md)): the tick costs **104s mean / 125s max** (18 ticks, one process — a reading, not a distribution) split 51.7% `run_one_tick` / 46.8% `order_monitor` at `attributed_pct` 98.5, so decoupling the monitor makes its own **52.8s peak** the cadence floor — clearing 60s by 7s, **necessary AND barely sufficient**. Tier-2 proposal: look inside the monitor's 48.7s BEFORE building the loop (a 14-phase split is built and unmerged in #8756), and set any budget from a real soak, never from that 18-tick reading. **THE DECOUPLE IS BUILT, MERGED, DEPLOYED AND LIVE-VERIFIED 2026-08-12** ([`S-M20-EXIT-LOOP-DECOUPLE-2026-08-12`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M20-EXIT-LOOP-DECOUPLE-2026-08-12.md), Tier-2 operator-approved; PRs #8778 → `64ee435`, #8807 → `3c6eaf9`, #8810 → `180cb1d`) — the proposal's own precondition was honoured first (the 14-phase split landed and IS what made the decision decidable). **Exit evaluation now runs on its own daemon loop at an observed 30.2s period** (`EXIT_LOOP_INTERVAL_SECONDS` 30, period-targeting so the inter-evaluation interval is `max(interval, pass)`), versus once per ~96s tick before: a **3.2× rate increase**, worst observed interval **34.1s**, clearing the 60s ask by **25.87s (43.1%)**. Only the EXIT half moved; the 13 reconcilers stay on the tick. Rollback is one env flip, no redeploy (`EXIT_LOOP_DECOUPLE_DISABLED=1`). ⚠️ **THE THREE FIGURES IN THE SENTENCES ABOVE ARE SUPERSEDED AND MUST NOT BE RE-QUOTED** — they came from **n=7/n=18 in one process**, and at **n=172** the two-halves finding reproduced almost exactly (48.4% / 51.1%, children÷parent 99.5%) while **both maxes moved against the proposal**: the monitor's peak is **61.82s, so whole-monitor-on-a-thread FAILS 60s outright** rather than "clearing it by 7s, barely sufficient", and the exit half alone clears by **41.8%, not 53%**. PRs #9856/#9858/#9859. **2026-08-20 (late) — THE MILESTONE IS RENAMED AND THE BRACKET IS MEASURED NOT TO BE A DECISION** (`docs/research/e35-bracket-is-not-a-decision-2026-08-20.md`; tools `scripts/research/e35_barrier_race.py` 39/39 self-test + `e35_bracket_geometry_sweep.py` 44/44). Operator directive (§ E3.6): *"the brackets should be predictive … we're really not just looking for exit points anymore, we're looking for how to correctly manage trades actively"*. **MEASURED — population: 6,428 trades, 19 donchian/pullback/squeeze legs, config-exact base, full history 2021-08-16→2026-08-19, NET OF FEES** (candles fetched this session from data.binance.vision, free lane, trainer VM NOT used; SOL/XRP/ADA/AVAX each miss 5 days of a 1,830-day span = 0.27%, an upstream archive gap): **16 of 19 legs declare `tp_r: 50.0`**, so the take-profit actually placed is `entry × 1.099` — *the exchange's rejection threshold*, not a decision. Because that is a fixed fraction of PRICE, **`tp_R` and `ATR/close` are the same variable** (`collinearity_check` `confirmed` 19/19, worst deviation 2.78e-17), so the venue clamp silently imports a volatility filter nobody designed — `tp_R < 0.75` is *exactly* `ATR/close > 5.28%`. Target distance varies **6.5×–38.9× WITHIN every leg** (medians cross-checked **18/18 EXACT, worst |diff| 0.0** against the harnesses' independently-computed `tp_r_effective_median`). **The barrier race:** P(TP first) is monotone in `tp_R` (49.1% → 45.2% → 34.5% → 18.9% → 10.1% → 3.2%) while **P(stop) is nearly flat** (32.1–39.1%) — the race is decided almost entirely by where the venue put the target. **76.2% of fleet net R (206.91 of 271.56) comes from the 23.1% of trades whose target is >5R away**, i.e. from trades the bracket CANNOT close. The race-inverted population (`tp_R<0.75`, target nearer than the stop) wins its race **49.1%** of the time and still loses **−0.168 R/trade** — negative on **8 of the 11 legs** that have any, ⚠️ but n=106 pooled (1.6%) at per-leg n 1–21, so **no single leg supports a claim** and the pooled direction is all that is claimed. ⚠️⚠️ **THIS AXIS CANNOT SEPARATE "bad bracket geometry" from "loses in high vol"** — per the collinearity they are one variable; separating them needs `atr_stop_mult` to VARY, which is the bracket-geometry sweep (in flight at session end, result NOT back-read into this row). Also corrected in-session: the sweep tool's first stop-axis rule refused cells on a net_R-vs-fixed-notional sign disagreement — **backwards**, since `position_size` sizes by RISK, so `net_R` is the account's unit; measured on `ada_pullback_2h` the refused cells (`sm3`: +17.23 R vs base +14.98) needed **less** notional per unit risk budget (13.16 vs 15.89), so the rule refused the desirable direction. Replaced by a reported-never-gated `leverage_multiple`. the m20 gate reused BY IMPORT, a new coverage-matrix DIMENSION not a new gate) — POPULATION: 19 legs x 199 cells = **3,781 measured cells + 133 gate rows**, config-exact base, tp_cap 0.099, oos-trades target 50, folds 2021-2026, NET OF FEES, span 2021-08-16..2026-08-19. **The dimension is NOT flat**: spread min 33.9 / median **66.8** / max 329.3 R — so the pre-registered null (*'if net R barely moves the exit dimension has little to give'*) does NOT hold. **But it is mostly overfitting, and the argmax is the max of 199 draws**: 112 of 133 gate rows are `is_oos_fail` before a fold runs, and `xrp_pullback_2h` has a 70.20 R spread with an argmax that beats base by **EXACTLY 0.00 R**. ⚠️ **The 17 `wf_pass` is INFLATED — 20 INERT fold-wins across 8 cells** (BL-20260817): four cells recording 6/6 are **2/6-3/6 EFFECTIVE**, all on the timeout or tp axis; and 8 of the 17 are `eth_pullback_2h` + `eth_pullback_prop_2h`, **the same underlying leg**, so read n=1. **THE ONE RESULT: E3's combine-don't-isolate falsifier is MET for the first time.** The JOINT cell `tp2_sm3.5_to48` on `eth_pullback_2h` gains **+3.962 R OOS** at target 50 vs the best SINGLE cell's **+0.397**, and is **`split_sensitive: FALSE` at pass_fraction 1.0** across five targets while that single cell **flips sign** (+0.454 -> -0.365) and fails 3 of 5;
