Candles fetched on the **free lane; the trainer VM was not used.** **READINESS MEASURED 2026-08-22** ([`m20-readiness-2026-08-22`](docs/research/m20-readiness-2026-08-22.md)): the done-condition matrix is **399 of 468 cells = 85.3% resolved**, and the 69 remaining are **three different kinds of thing** that must not be scheduled together — **25 INFRA** (`bracket_geometry :: blocked:no_free_lane_candle_feed`, buildable now), **20 WORK** (`pending`, ordinary pipeline runs), **15 WAITING** (soak depth, nothing accelerates it), 9 other. ⚠️ **The biggest blocker is a missing artifact, not research**: those 25 cells are **36% of everything remaining and 51% of everything blocked**, and the free lane (`data.binance.vision`) is a **crypto** archive while all 25 blocked legs are non-crypto (IBKR futures x5, US equities/ETFs x18, XAUUSD) — a source-coverage fact, not a forgotten backlog item. **The same feed closes M31 P5 precondition 3b**, so one artifact unblocks two threads the roadmap lists apart. Source **decided: yfinance** (operator, 2026-08-22); missing pieces are `1d` support (21 of the 25 legs are `1d`/`1h`) and the `_yf_ticker` map, which lives in the dashboard repo. ⚠️ **Prove it on a GitHub runner** — Yahoo returns HTTP 429 from the sandbox. Sequenced **after** the open CRITICAL exit/protection rows, per the same operator decision. ⚠️ **STRUCTURAL FINDING ON M20's OWN PREMISE (2026-08-24, the first `review_coverage.structural_health` pass — measured over ALL 1324 closed non-backtest trades, whole history):** the M20 levers have fired **17 times EVER — 1.3%** (`stale_stop` 10, `exit_head` 4, `giveback_stop` 3), and **857 of 1324 closes (64.7%) come from cleanup machinery** (reconciler / orphan / netting / half-open sweeps) rather than from a decision. The charitable reading was checked and only half holds: of the 580 `reconciler_filled` rows, 326 (56.2%) DO read a real broker fill — a bracket firing that the reconciler merely recorded, which is healthy — but the remaining 254 got `candle_at_close`/`local_markprice`, i.e. the position was gone and a price was reconstructed. **So the exit-refinement pipeline is being built on a path that rarely executes**, and per **M39(A)** the decided path is also the one carrying no provenance (27.0% measured vs the janitor path's 52.0%) — meaning an M20 lever result on those legs cannot currently be falsified. This does not invalidate M20's research (the harness sweeps are backtests, not journal reads); it bounds what the LIVE soaks can prove, and it sequences **M39(A) before further live lever flips**. **B4 SHIPPED 2026-08-29 — the first bracket-geometry cells reach live config** ([#10419](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10419), merged `91de68b9`, **Tier-3, operator-approved twice: the item, then the reduced scope**). 14 fields on 8 legs: `spy`/`qqq`/`iwm`/`scha_trend_long_1d`, `mgc_pullback_1d`, `uso_trend_1h` carry `tp_r` + `atr_stop_mult`; `tlt_pullback_1h` and `slv_trend_1h` carry the stop only. Selection rule stated rather than improvised — highest `wf_wins_effective`, tie-break `d_net_r`, over `wf_pass`/`path_b_wf_pass` cells — with every value traced to `docs/research/e35-bracket-corpus.jsonl` and each edit carrying its cell/verdict/wf inline in the YAML. Verified on `main` by counting the annotations (14). Four `test_yaml_entries_pin_validated_params` tests failed and were RIGHT to: they pin the 2026-06-20 sweeps' geometry, which this supersedes; the pins were UPDATED to the new validated values, never loosened, and only for legs B4 changed. ⚠️ **TWO CLAIMS NARROWED, both of which shaped the decision to do B4 first.** (1) B4 was recommended over the smaller lever cells because bracket geometry is *"the only lever that declares a target"* — it does that on **6** legs, not 14: 5 of the 14 winning cells are stop-or-timeout-only. (2) The 6 unshipped legs are **not** blocked on wiring but **unshippable by construction**: their winning cells carry a timeout component, the trend/pullback HARNESSES model `timeout_bars` (`backtest_trend.py:497`, `backtest_pullback.py:496`, default 200) and the LIVE units implement no bar-count exit at all (`trend_donchian.py` has zero occurrences; `htf_pullback_trend_2h.py` had a docstring claiming a backstop no code implements, corrected in [#10420](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10420)). So the coverage matrix's `passed_unshipped` column currently conflates *awaiting approval* with *impossible to ship* — new workplan row **B9**, `BL-20260829-HARNESS-FORCE-CLOSES-TREND-PULLBACK-TRADES-ON-BAR-COUNT-AND-LIVE-NEVER-DOES`. ⚠️ **B6 is no longer independent of B4**: two of its cells are trail3 on `tlt_pullback_1h` / `uso_trend_1h`, whose `atr_stop_mult` B4 just moved — validated at the old stop, so re-sweep before shipping. Plan: [`docs/claude/WORKPLAN-2026-08-29.md`](docs/claude/WORKPLAN-2026-08-29.md) Lane B. **B9 ANSWERED + B10/D1b/B6-prep + the re-sweep, night of 2026-08-29** ([`S-NIGHT-N3-N7-E35-RESWEEP-2026-08-29`](docs/sprint-logs/S-NIGHT-N3-N7-E35-RESWEEP-2026-08-29.md), plan [`WORKPLAN-NIGHT-2026-08-29.md`](docs/claude/WORKPLAN-NIGHT-2026-08-29.md)). **D1b concluded — RECOMMENDATION: do NOT build a live bar-count exit** ([`docs/design/d1b-live-time-stop-PROPOSAL.md`](docs/design/d1b-live-time-stop-PROPOSAL.md), [#10438](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10438)); ⚠️ it also corrects the workplan's own population from **4 legs to 2**. An honest negative, recorded so it is not re-litigated. **B10 SHIPPED** — the `matrix-bracket-values` guard ([#10437](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10437)) now asserts a matrix cell id's named axes against the leg's LIVE `config/strategies.yaml`, closing the gap that let B4 ship geometry to 8 live legs while all 8 read `passed_unshipped` and `matrix-config-agreement` stayed green; three states, never collapsed, with `unreadable` a FAILURE and a `to*` component permanently `undeliverable_axis`. **B6 split prepared** ([#10439](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10439)): the 12 `passed_unshipped` cells are 4 bracket-geometry (B4's) + exactly 8 B6 cells splitting 6+2; B9's precondition holds on 5 of 6 named legs, and `ict_scalp_sol_15m` plus 2 shadow rows are ABSENT from the corpus — **ungraded, not clean**. **The 41-leg re-sweep landed** (corpus 8,211 → 8,289 keys, 0 lost, [#10441](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10441)) after [#10440](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/Metis-Insights/pull/10440) stopped the workflow retargeting a constant long-diverged branch that discarded every row. ⚠️ **ITS OWN CLEAN-LEG CONTROL FAILED and NO VERDICT CHANGE HAS BEEN APPLIED**: 3,515 of 4,583 clean-leg cells differ (76.7%), 18 of 23 legs. Diagnosed as the control being *unsatisfiable* — the sweep fetches a trailing window (`days=1830` ending at run time), so both edges move — on fold-level evidence over **only 11 of the 3,515** differing cells; state that denominator. The PIN's own reading is separate and positive: **0** previously-CLEAN legs regressed, 10 of 10 residual `base!=to400` legs were already contaminated, 8 contaminated legs now show `base==to400`. **Deferred on the operator's ruling:** 19 matrix cells whose ref still cites the superseded 08-20 run (2 asserting `passed_unshipped` on a winner the newer rows do not reproduce), and the 9 reverse-direction legs — deliberately unread, since 7 of 9 are timeout-contaminated and were being re-measured. `OI-20260829-E35-RESWEEP-LANDED-BUT-ITS-CLEAN-LEG-CONTROL-FAILED`. **MATRIX RE-CHECKED 2026-08-29 on the operator's ruling** ([`S-E35-MATRIX-RECHECK-2026-08-29`](docs/sprint-logs/S-E35-MATRIX-RECHECK-2026-08-29.md), [`e35-matrix-recheck-2026-08-29.md`](docs/research/e35-matrix-recheck-2026-08-29.md)) — the operator ACCEPTED the fold-level substitute for the failed clean-leg control, which authorised the reading and NOT a config change. **Stale refs 30 → 0** (the earlier '19' is not reproducible from what was recorded; the definition is now executable in `scripts/research/e35_matrix_recheck.py`). ✅ **B4 is OUTCOME-CONFIRMED, and the check that confirms it is the one that looked alarming**: all 8 shipped legs show ZERO passing cells, which reads as regression and is instead the expected signature of an absorbed lever — the base is config-exact, so the winner cannot beat itself. The base ROSE by the improvement each shipped cell claimed: **3 of 8 exact to four decimal places, all 8 within 0.89–1.03×, median 1.00**. ⚠️ Control denominator: only 10 legs carry both runs and the 2 non-B4 ones are byte-identical on all 199 cells (`PROXY_DATA` maps MGC *and* XAUUSD to `GC_F`), so it is **n=1 independent leg** — it moved DOWN while all 8 moved up. **The 2 ETH `passed_unshipped` cells are UNSHIPPABLE, not merely stale** — both winners carry a `to48` axis no live unit can deliver, decisive without the re-measurement, and both now measure negative; 2 MORE of the same shape were found that the earlier note missed (`gld_pullback_1h`, `spy_pullback_1h`), whose winners are corrected rather than blocked. **DIRECTION REVERSED on 10 legs** (the B9 mechanism: prior verdicts were measured against a fictional bar-count exit) yielding **15 shippable gate-passing cells — a TIER-3 PROPOSAL SET, NOT APPLIED** (`OI-20260829-E35-REVERSED-LEGS-ARE-A-TIER-3-PROPOSAL-SET-NOT-APPLIED`). ⚠️ Do not re-quote the earlier "9 reverse legs, sol holds 7" — measured on the contaminated pre-re-sweep corpus; it is 10 legs and sol holds 2 shippable. ⚠️ **12 matrix legs carry no corpus rows at all — ungraded, not clean.** 🚀 **SHIPPED 2026-08-30 — the reversed geometry is LIVE on 9 of the 10 approved legs (Tier-3, operator-approved in conversation); the 10th was HELD on evidence** ([`S-E35-SHIP-REVERSED-GEOMETRY-2026-08-30`](docs/sprint-logs/S-E35-SHIP-REVERSED-GEOMETRY-2026-08-30.md)). ⚠️ **15 CELLS IS NOT 15 CHANGES** — a leg carries ONE geometry, so the 15 candidates resolve to **10 legs / 11 field edits, of which 9 legs / 10 edits SHIPPED** under the selection rule stated in ADVANCE and identical to B4 (highest `wf_wins_effective`, tie-break `d_net_r`, shippable cells only). Both apparent ties are ILLUSORY and self-verifying: identical `d_net_r` because the leg already declares the other axis. **THREE legs route to `bybit_2` = REAL MONEY** (`trend_donchian` `sm2`, `trend_donchian_eth_4h` `sm2` wf 6/6, `trend_donchian_xrp_4h` `tp3_sm2`); two are `execution: shadow`; one is prop. ⚠️ **TWO CHANGES ALTER STRATEGY SHAPE, NOT STOP WIDTH, and one is real money** — `ada_pullback_2h` and `trend_donchian_xrp_4h` move `tp_r` **50.0 → 4.0 / 3.0**, introducing a take-profit where the leg had NONE (50 is the no-target sentinel); capping a TREND leg at 3R truncates exactly the rare large winners trend-following lives on. It passed its walk-forward and was operator-approved; it is the highest-variance change in the set. ⚠️ **`avax_pullback_2h` (+1.0084) shipped against the written recommendation to DROP it** — operator reaffirmed the full set after reading that; `execution: shadow`, so nothing live moves. ⚠️ **Every cell rests on a run whose CLEAN-leg control FAILED**, on the fold-level substitute accepted 2026-08-29 — a weaker base than B4's. ⚠️ **`trend_donchian_eth_prop` was NOT shipped** — its `sm1.5` cell is real but it is a config-only EXIT variant whose premise is ENTRY-IDENTITY with `trend_donchian_eth`, it was graduated live on a PROP EV/survival gate (`run_ev_montecarlo` under `breakout.yaml`, +$883 @ P=0.8477 / 1,110 trades) measured on that construction, and `breakout_1` can be PERMANENTLY DISABLED by a breach; a stop change moves the R denominator, the size and the breach probability. Caught by `test_unit_c_prop_exit_variants`, which was RIGHT and was NOT weakened; recorded `blocked:prop_ev_gate_would_be_invalidated` with its unblock condition. ✅ The 9 shipped cells are now `shipped`, so **`matrix-bracket-values` enforces all 17 shipped cells** against `config/strategies.yaml` — a silent drift back is now a CI failure. ⚠️ **DEPLOYED, NOT PROVEN**: no `/api/bot/config` read has yet shown the values on the running trader and no trade has closed under them (`OI-20260830-E35-GEOMETRY-SHIPPED-TO-9-LEGS-NOT-YET-LIVE-VERIFIED`, loud, 2-day cadence). A test coupling was caught rather than broken: `eth_pullback_2h`'s pin to the BTC leg's `atr_stop_mult` is now per-leg with its own evidence, never loosened — ETH stays 2.5 because its own verdict is `blocked:no_live_bar_count_exit`. |

Diagnosis: small-TF fails on (1) **fee-drag** (backtests charge 7.5bps **taker** on both legs; live path is 100% market/taker; Bybit **maker** ≈ 0/rebate) and (2) **OHLCV-blindness** (the edge lives in order-flow, which the features can't see). Four operator-approved directions: **P1** maker-fee economics re-scorer (do cells flip positive at maker fees? → post-only entry build if yes), **P2** start the forward-only order-flow capture clock (OFI/VPIN/micro-price; pipeline built, data-starved), **P3** market-neutral sleeve backtests (funding-carry / cross-sectional momentum — change the edge), **P4** P(win) entry filter attempt-3 (GATED on a genuinely new input — order-flow features or a net-R label — NOT another OHLCV run; the M18/M21 P_win efforts failed twice). P4 stays gated. **P1b RESOLVED (trainer #6493, PR #6494):** the neutral funding-carry re-priced at maker (1.0bps) recovers ~70% of the gross funding harvest with tiny drawdown (ETH +0.70→+7.72R, SOL +0.98→+6.46R, BNB −1.87→+11.03R) — maker execution IS the lever — BUT the current-regime OOS (2025-01+) maker net is thin-to-negative (ETH +0.40/SOL +0.07/BNB −0.61): a real but **regime-gated** candidate, parked with a re-trigger (`PB-20260715-MAKER-CARRY`), not live-now. **Wave-2 directions (operator-approved 2026-07-15, running):** **D1** frequency-reduction gates (session/killzone re-scorer #6497 + HTF-regime confluence), **D2** stop-free carry cousins — **HEADLINE FINDING: market-neutral crypto cointegration PAIRS at 1h are the reliable tool** (#6498/#6500/#6501): all 4 pairs robustly net-positive, **fee-insensitive** (R normalized by the wide spread-stop dwarfs fees), and — unlike the funding carry — **OOS-validated on held-out 2025-26** with NO expectancy decay (SOL/BTC OOS +175R exp 0.258 / BNB/BTC +177R / SOL/ETH +163R / ETH/BTC +158R; OOS win 58-60%, OOS maxDD 6-15R). Sidesteps BOTH root causes. **Risk validations PASSED (#6506/#6509):** capital-efficiency ~0.8-1.2 net_R/pos-day (OOS 0.93-1.12, the operator's original metric — beats scalps/cash); cointegration STABLE (half-life ~12h, mean-reverting in 100% of 30-day windows over 3yr, no breaks → maxDD is bounded excursion not structural blow-up); perp-funding NEGLIGIBLE (~0.5 bps/trade ≈ 2-4% of the edge). **Tier-3 proposal DRAFTED** ([`docs/research/pairs-sleeve-PROPOSAL-2026-07-15.md`](docs/research/pairs-sleeve-PROPOSAL-2026-07-15.md)): evidence + the architectural lift (a market-neutral pair is a 2-LEG strategy that doesn't fit the single-symbol intent model → needs a new isolated pairs-executor primitive, the main Tier-3 risk) + a phased shadow→demo(bybit_1)→real(bybit_2) rollout + mandatory account_compat_matrix + open operator decisions. Remaining before live: the $-on-2-leg-margin sizing (a proposal choice) + execution realism (2-leg fills, only a live/paper soak settles it). **Drafted, not self-wired — operator-gated.** **D1a** session/killzone gating (#6499) = marginal/negative (scalps structurally fee-dead; D1b regime-confluence deprioritized). **D3** passive LP sleeve (gated on P2 order-flow accrual), **D4** new signal inputs (funding/OI features; cross-venue/on-chain parked). **D2 pairs sleeve BUILT + LIVE on paper (2026-07-15, operator-approved):** the isolated 2-leg pairs executor (`src/units/strategies/pairs_executor.py::run_pairs_tick`, NOT via `multi_account_execute`) + parity-verified signal engine + β-hedged sizing + observe-only soak (`/api/bot/pairs/soak`) shipped in #6518/#6519, and all 4 pairs flipped `execution: shadow → live` on `bybit_1` (Bybit demo/paper venue) in #6521 — so they now place real 2-leg orders on paper money to soak live==backtest before any real-money (`bybit_2`) promotion. So a live flip-override IS running, and it has NOT beaten `hold` out-of-sample — no walk-forward has ever tested a positive threshold. The approval is recorded; the **evidence gate stays OPEN** and P3's job changes from *propose enabling* to *measure the arm already running* (`BL-20260811-FLIP-OVERRIDE-NEVER-WALKFORWARDED`). This correction exists because the row asserted a discipline the system was not keeping, and a roadmap that overstates its own gate-keeping is worse than one that admits an exception. ✅ **RESOLVED SAME DAY 2026-08-11 — measured, then disarmed.** P3 did its changed job: the walk-forward ran ([`flip-override-walkforward-2026-08-11`](docs/research/flip-override-walkforward-2026-08-11.md), PR #8783) and the live `0.15 / 4.0` arm **LOSES** — −$1,280 over 34 fires (79% of net), never improving drawdown in any cell, worse on both axes in both larger-sample halves. Operator granted Tier-3 approval to disarm; `FLIP_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD=0.0` went live **20:14Z** (#8785) and was verified on the running process, not just the `.env` — `/proc/<MainPID>/environ` reads `0.0` (#8787) and the trader is ticking with a 42s heartbeat (#8788). **Live routing is plain `hold` again, so the sentence this correction flagged is true once more** — and now it is true *because it was measured*, not by assumption. ✅ **TF-class split ANSWERED, same day — no re-arm warranted** (`BL-20260811-FLIP-OVERRIDE-TFCLASS-REARM` closed, run `31529876774`). Cross-clock-only loses to plain `hold` in **3 of 4** cells (aggregate $1,031 vs $1,622 = **−$591**) and **worsens maxDD in both train halves**; its lone winning cell is B/OOS on **one fire** inside a book that loses either way. Same-clock-only loses in **all four** ($683 vs $1,622). What the run DOES confirm is M26 P0's prediction that cross-TF is the *less bad* class — cross-only beats the TF-blind arm by +$689 — **and that is precisely the comparison the re-arm bar refuses**: the blind arm loses, so a narrower arm that merely loses less has cleared nothing. Ranking within a family of losing arms does not produce a winner. The bar was fixed in writing before the run for exactly this reason. **P3 is closed on the confidence-gap knobs**; a future re-arm needs a fresh walk-forward clearing plain `hold` on net without worsening drawdown. |

The `vix_term` **S4** recommendation is *do not productionize as a standalone signal* (thin edge, large drawdowns) — at most a low-weight conviction/exposure tilt, Tier-3 operator-gated. **VALUE-CONSTRUCTION P4 SWEEP RAN 2026-07-27 (entry 13, PR #7766/#7773):** D1/D2/D3 value constructions graded through the **P4 net-of-cost LIFECYCLE gate** (`value_construction_sweep.py`, the arbiter the directive names — distinct from entry 12's S2/S3 grade). **Nothing clears** (`edge_vs_baseline > 0`): every cell still loses to naive all-long net-of-cost over 21yr. BUT D1 `change`/`detrend` + D2 `turning` **beat the level baseline AND flip conviction-calibration positive** (`change`: calib +0.0224, win 0.515, edge −0.0031 vs baseline −0.0047) — confirming "edge lives in the *shift*, not the *level*"; sub-threshold but the first directional value signal. **C4 exit-conditioning stays un-warranted** (no edge-positive base thesis to protect). **VALUE NOW CROSS-GATE-CONCLUSIVELY EXHAUSTED (entry 16, PR #7777, 2026-07-27):** the queued D4/D5/D2-regime iteration RAN through the P4 gate (21yr backfill + real ETF candles) and **nothing clears** — every cell still loses to naive all-long net-of-cost at every horizon and in every regime. The D4 **equal-weight composite** (`change ⊕ detrend`) is the program's **best-calibrated construction** (calib **+0.0549**, edge −0.0034) yet still sub-benchmark; **IC-weighting did WORSE** than equal-weight (edge −0.0050); the **D5 horizon sweep** shows `change`'s edge negative at every horizon and monotonically worse with the hold (7d −0.0025 → 180d −0.0342); **D2 regime** (calm-vol / up-trend) doesn't rescue it. So the "value exhausted" verdict now holds under **BOTH arbiters** (S2/S3 horizon-IC, entry 12; P4 net-of-cost lifecycle, entries 13+16) across the **FULL** construction space (D1/D2/D3/D4/D5 + regime). Value construction is **closed**. **Recommended pivot** (per the 2026-07-24 Escalation, already underway; operator-endorsed 2026-07-27): higher-freq microstructure off existing feeds (M30/M36-D, no new cost) or an operator-gated non-FRED dataset (Schwab options-skew Track B / paid).
