   0.611 market-only → 0.680 with owner/cell, per-fold min ≥ 0.55 on every stack (no knife-edge),
   materially above the 06-30 ≈ 0.51. **But the decisive sizing-normalized concurrency-capped selection
   backtest FAILED:** the learned-ranker arm (net −586) lost to both dumb symbol-priority (−547;
   `learned_beats_priority_net=false`, −$39) and the incumbent rules EV scorer (−375; −$211). The AUC is
   between-owner base-rate, **not** a within-tick cross-symbol selection edge. **Decision:** keep the rules
   EV scorer as the selector; **M18 P2/P3 stay PARKED**; the learned-ranker-as-selector track is CLOSED.

### Post-value pivot — the two next directions (operator-endorsed 2026-07-27)

With the M28 value sleeve **cross-gate-conclusively exhausted** (ledger entry 16 /
PR #7777 — the full D1/D2/D3/D4/D5 + regime construction space graded through BOTH
arbiters, zero cost-surviving edges), the program pivots to two genuinely new
**input classes** (the exhausted work varied *construction*; these vary the
*input*). Both operator-endorsed 2026-07-27. Consolidating sequencing workplan +
decision gates: [`POST-VALUE-PIVOT-WORKPLAN-2026-07-27.md`](docs/research/POST-VALUE-PIVOT-WORKPLAN-2026-07-27.md).
Anchor **`MB-20260727-POST-VALUE-PIVOT`**.

1. **Track 1 — higher-frequency microstructure (PRIMARY, unblocked, Tier-1, no cost).**
   Home = **M30** (platform built + validated) + **M36 Track D** (integration
   backbone). Discovery runs backtest-first (large N; the live journal is row-
   starved), **exit-timing + regime/session-conditioned studies first** (the
   load-bearing prior: entries are ~coin-flip OOS, edge lives in exit/regime), and
   every result feeds the **M16 conviction master model**. Starts next session; the
   turnkey ordered study queue is the **M30 deep-research session prompt**
   ([`M30-deep-research-SESSION-PROMPT.md`](docs/research/M30-deep-research-SESSION-PROMPT.md)).
2. **Track 2 — Schwab options-implied skew (PARALLEL, ONE operator hand-off).**
   Home = **M31 Track B**; the credential-free pipeline is already built + tested.
   A forward-looking class overlapping live legs (SPY/QQQ/DIA→MES/MNQ, GLD→gold).
   **Blocked only on the operator registering the Schwab dev app + adding
   `SCHWAB_APP_KEY`/`SCHWAB_APP_SECRET` to Actions secrets** (then a weekly OAuth
   re-auth). The moment the secret lands, the soak→grade path is plug-and-play
   ([`M31-track-b-soak-plan.md`](docs/research/M31-track-b-soak-plan.md)); it accrues
   ~1 quarter to the first honest read, so it runs in the background alongside Track 1.

**Definition of done (both tracks):** a construction clears the standing net-of-cost
walk-forward gate OOS (→ a Tier-3 productionization proposal) OR the input class is
mapped as carrying no cost-surviving edge — both are recorded results in the relevant
ledger. Live only via Tier-3 operator approval.

### Cross-session priority order — macro / signal-R&D track (adopted 2026-07-25, operator-directed)

One authoritative work-order so **concurrent sessions can each claim one lane without
colliding** (the lanes are file-disjoint; claim on the coordination board before starting).

### M18 — Portfolio Capital Allocator (AI) (phased proposal)

> **Status:** 📋 PROPOSED 2026-06-29 (research/design session) — Tier-3, **PROPOSE-ONLY**. No
> `src/`, `config/`, or live-path file changed by the proposal. Full design (inventory → gaps →
> proposed models/datasets/allocator → shadow-soak → graduation gates):
> [`docs/research/capital-allocation-ai-DESIGN.md`](docs/research/capital-allocation-ai-DESIGN.md).
> Execution/delegation breakdown (units S-M18-P0a..P4, tiers, parallelization, Mode-C spawn prompts):
> [`docs/sprint-plans/M18-CAPITAL-ALLOCATOR-EXECUTION-PLAN.md`](docs/sprint-plans/M18-CAPITAL-ALLOCATOR-EXECUTION-PLAN.md).
> Every phase before P4 is observe-only; nothing influences a live order until a backtest A/B
> passes and the operator approves. The allocator only **selects + reduces** — the per-account
> `RiskManager` stays the final sizing authority (Prime Directive); graduation rides a `*_MODE`
> flag (`off`/`annotate`/`apply`), never a default-off `*_ENABLED` gate.
>
> **Backtest findings 2026-06-30 (P0–P1 substrate built + soaked + A/B-tested; P2 selector NOT
> justified on current evidence — PARKED):** the observe-only substrate shipped (P0a cost capture
> #5099, P0b candidate batch #5086, P0c soak+regret #5092, P1 EV scorer #5098, BT arm #5107). Three
> independent backtests then tested whether the EV scorer's **selection** beats dumb priority and
> found it does **not**: (1) intra-symbol `--allocator off` vs `ev` ≈ 96–97% identical; (2) the
> **sizing-normalized** cross-symbol A/B (`shared_priority` control) — `ev − priority = −$7`, so the
> apparent cross-symbol edge was **sizing/concentration, not selection**; (3) a 1,559-candidate
> walk-forward ranker reached only **OOS AUC ≈ 0.51** (no decision-time feature separates winners,
> `ev_r` is below chance). → **Do not build the P2/P3 selector on the current confidence-proxy
> scorer.** The only untested `P_win` input is the ML-head/per-cell-expectancy conviction stack
> (P3), which must be proven in the sizing-normalized harness before any routing plumbing. Evidence:
> [`docs/research/M18-allocator-backtest-findings-2026-06-29.md`](docs/research/M18-allocator-backtest-findings-2026-06-29.md);
> research tooling under `scripts/research/allocator_{multisymbol_backtest,candidate_dataset,ranker_eval}.py`.
> All Tier-3 decisions parked.

**The problem:** the pipeline is per-strategy/per-account independent — the intent multiplexer
collapses all candidates to one `DesiredPosition`/symbol (`intent_multiplexer.py:616`), fanned to
accounts where each `RiskManager.position_size` sizes independently (`risk.py:620`). No global step
ranks the full opportunity set by cost-aware EV and picks the capital-constrained best subset.

**The seam already exists:** `AllocatorInterface.allocate(signals, PortfolioState)` + a dormant
`PassthroughAllocator` (`src/core/allocator.py`, "no portfolio caps … introduced in later sprints")
behind `CENTRALIZED_ALLOCATOR` — fed `[one_sig]` today. M18 writes the smart impl and feeds it the
full batch.

| Phase | Scope | Tier | Graduation gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P0 — Substrate + soak** | Tier-1 per-trade **cost capture** (`fee_*`/`funding_*` on the close path — closes the #1 data gap blocking the learned ranker); expose the candidate batch the multiplexer already gathers; `allocator_soak.jsonl` + `/api/bot/allocator/soak` + a **regret** metric (EV/realized-R the allocator would have captured vs the per-cell path actually did) | T1 | Soak accruing; cost columns populating; regret computable. No order influence. |
| **P1 — Rules EV scorer** | `allocator_ev.py`: cost-aware `EV_net = P_win·R_target − (1−P_win)·R_stop − roundtrip_fee − funding/swap` per candidate (P_win ← conviction lens; fixed cost model until P0 capture matures), stamped on `SignalPackage.source_context`; soak-only | T1 | EV logged per candidate; sanity vs realized R. |
| **P2 — Greedy selector (shadow)** | `EVAllocator(AllocatorInterface)`: greedy EV-per-unit-risk subset under an enriched `PortfolioState` (free margin + daily-loss budget remaining + max-concurrent); **annotate** (logs the chosen subset, executes nothing) | T3 | Soak shows positive realized regret reduction + budgets bind often enough to matter. |
| **P3 — Correlation budgeting + learned ranker** | covariance-adjusted marginal-risk term (the missing correlation input, also a logged feature); a trained **expected-net-R** ranker on the candidate→shadow→advisory ladder (LightGBM, new ranker task family) | T3 | Ranker beats rules EV in shadow + RG-style robustness; clean cost labels (P0) in hand. |
| **P4 — Graduate to influence** | backtest A/B (allocator vs per-cell baseline) on net-of-cost PnL + maxDD; live behind `CAPITAL_ALLOCATOR_MODE=off\|annotate\|apply` | T3 | A/B PASS (net PnL, then maxDD%, OOS ≥ in-sample, regret-reduction > 0); prop arm clears `run_ev_montecarlo` survival; operator approval; rollback = `off`. |

**Honest gaps (full table in the design doc):** no N-way capital-constrained selector; no live
correlation/covariance; fees/funding are backtest-only, **not logged per live trade** (the binding
data gap — must land in P0 before the P3 ranker is trained); decision-time features (`model_scores`,
`account_context_snapshots`, conviction inputs) and net-R labels are otherwise mostly present.

### M19 — AI Model Strategy (new model types) (phased proposal)

> **Status:** 📋 PROPOSED 2026-07-01 (research/design session) — **PROPOSE-ONLY**. No
> `src/`, `config/`, `ml/`, or live-path file changed by the proposal. Full design
> (reframe → current-state inventory → the capability ladder → compute-tier
> economics → the "reads-everything" encoder → trade-offs → gates → phase
> checklist): [`docs/research/ai-model-strategy-roadmap-2026-07-01.md`](docs/research/ai-model-strategy-roadmap-2026-07-01.md).
> Successor to the largely-complete M14 optimization program. Every model type
> graduates observe-only through `candidate → shadow → advisory`; nothing
> influences a live order until a backtest A/B passes and the operator approves.

**The reframe:** every model we run today is a *narrow tabular classifier over
hand-engineered features* (46 manifests: LightGBM regime/vol + trade-outcome +
setup-quality heads, one HMM, baselines) — they differ in target/symbol, not in
*kind*. M19 opens new model **types**. And the binding constraint is **labels
(~350 real trades), not compute** (the free 1-OCPU trainer clears the daily
cycle in <1h) → **self-supervised representation learning (label-free) is the
flagship**, and deep models enter as *encoders feeding the existing LightGBM
heads*, never end-to-end replacements.

**Compute tiers:** Tier 0 = **$0** free CPU (LightGBM/HMM/GMM + **frozen**
pretrained-TSFM CPU embeddings) — start now; Tier 1 = **~$10/mo** gated spot-GPU
bursts (deep sequence models + in-house self-supervised encoder v0; train on
GPU, **export inference to CPU/ONNX**) — spec'd now, spending gated on an operator
go-signal; Tier 2 = larger long-term spend (multi-task foundation encoder + RL).

| Phase | Type | Tier | Graduation gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| **T0.1 — frozen TSFM embeddings** | representation/embedding features (Chronos-Bolt-Tiny 9M, CPU `embed()`) fed into regime + conviction heads | T0 ($0) | Lifts regime AUC / conviction Brier in purged-CV vs no-embedding baseline. Observe-only. **✅ A/B RAN 2026-07-01 — POSITIVE (modest):** on a clean controlled A/B (base vs base+32 `tsfm_emb`, SAME dataset v003, 175k BTC-15m rows, chronos-bolt-tiny d_model 256 → seeded random-projection to 32), the frozen embeddings lift the regime head **macro_f1 +0.041 (0.576→0.617)** and **f1_volatile +0.030 (0.376→0.406)** with better volatile *precision* (+0.033) — a real signal beyond hand-engineered features, for $0 CPU. **✅ FOLLOW-UP CONFIRMED 2026-07-01 (3 levers):** (1) the lift **holds under purged walk-forward CV and generalizes** — BTC-15m Δmacro_f1 **+0.052** / Δf1_vol +0.035, ETH-15m **+0.058** / +0.016 (BTC-1h weak: +0.017 / flat, data-poorer); (2) **PCA ≈ random projection** (PCA-vs-random within ±0.001 — keep the simpler random projection); (3) **embeddings do NOT help the direction head** (+0.004 macro_f1 — the frozen representation carries *volatility* structure, not directional edge). Code (block `ml/datasets/embedding_features.py` + producer `scripts/ml/build_embeddings.py` + `market_features` `embedding_path` v9→v10 + PCA reduction + emb/control/pcv/direction manifests + 21 tests + trainer-side dep pins). Evidence: [`T0.1-embedding-AB-evidence-2026-07-01`](docs/research/T0.1-embedding-AB-evidence-2026-07-01.md) + follow-up [`T0.1-embedding-followup-evidence-2026-07-01`](docs/research/T0.1-embedding-followup-evidence-2026-07-01.md); log [`S-M19-T0.1-EMBEDDINGS-2026-07-01`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-T0.1-EMBEDDINGS-2026-07-01.md). **⚠️ BASE-RATE CLIFF (2026-07-01):** a `vol_threshold` sweep shows the lift is REAL across a **band** (0.003 +0.052 / 0.004 +0.037 macro_f1) and falls off a **cliff** at the shipped head's **0.005** (+0.012, f1_volatile flat) — driven by the volatile BASE RATE: at 4.6% (0.005) the class is too rare for EITHER model to learn (base f1_volatile 0.24), so embeddings can't help; at ≥8% (0.003–0.004) they clearly do. **Net — promotion isn't closed, it sharpens:** if the head's threshold is revisitable, 0.004 gives a better head (f1_vol 0.24→0.30) AND a lift → promote-candidate; if 0.005 is locked by the gate's semantics, embeddings don't help there → pivot to T0.2/T0.4. Separately, the shipped head being weak at its own 0.005 (f1_vol 0.24) is worth flagging to the ML/regime track independent of embeddings. Don't build Option-D until that threshold question is settled. Live-parity design ([`T0.1-live-parity-DESIGN`](docs/research/T0.1-live-parity-DESIGN.md)) is banked for when/if a head clears. Direction-head embeddings dropped. Evidence: [`T0.1-embedding-AB-evidence-2026-07-01`](docs/research/T0.1-embedding-AB-evidence-2026-07-01.md) + [`T0.1-embedding-followup-evidence-2026-07-01`](docs/research/T0.1-embedding-followup-evidence-2026-07-01.md); logs [`S-M19-T0.1-EMBEDDINGS-2026-07-01`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-T0.1-EMBEDDINGS-2026-07-01.md) + [`S-M19-EMBEDDINGS-FOLLOWUP-2026-07-01`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-EMBEDDINGS-FOLLOWUP-2026-07-01.md). |
| **T0.2 — unsupervised regime discovery** | GMM / clustering extending the lone HMM; discover states vs bucket vol | T0 | Beats the frozen-edge detector under the regime-router gate; shadow-only until then. **✅ A/B RAN 2026-07-01 — DOCUMENTED NEGATIVE:** ran the causal Gaussian-HMM (+ a forward-aware variant feeding the T0.4 `fc_range_rel` into the observation) vs the class-weighted LightGBM regime head, BTC-15m, purged-CV, at BOTH 0.005 (4.6% vol) and 0.003 (16% vol). The HMM **loses at both** (Δmacro_f1 −0.10 @ 0.005 collapsing to all-`range` f1_vol 0.0; −0.06 @ 0.003 barely surfacing volatile at recall 5%). Root cause: its state→label projection is an **unweighted argmax** → rare-class washout (the tree head avoids this via `class_weight: {volatile:28}`), and a 3-state Gaussian HMM is a weaker model than a class-weighted boosted tree on the same features. The **forward forecast HURTS the HMM** (destabilizes the Gaussian EM → full collapse) — the T0.2×T0.4 cross fails; `fc`'s value stays as a tree *feature* (T0.4), not an HMM emission. Matches the "illusion of regimes" dissent. **✅ SALVAGE 2026-07-01 — RESCUED TO NEAR-PARITY:** the one-line fix — class-weighting the HMM's state→label projection (new `label_projection_class_weight` knob, default off, PR #5371) — turns the collapsed model into one that surfaces volatile properly (recall 0.46 @ 0.005, 0.77 @ 0.003) and **essentially matches** the tree head: **macro_f1 within −0.007 @ 0.005 and −0.003 @ 0.003** (inside noise). So class-weighted unsupervised HMM regime discovery is **competitive, not superior** — a viable ensemble/alternative candidate (temporal persistence + interpretability the tree lacks), not an automatic promotion. **✅ ETH GENERALIZATION 2026-07-01 — DOES NOT HOLD → T0.2 CLOSES NEGATIVE:** mirrored the class-weighted HMM onto ETH — it's **−0.100 macro_f1** below the tree head (0.4552 vs 0.5553; over-predicts volatile, recall 0.83 / precision 0.14). The BTC near-parity was symbol-specific — the `balanced` weight over-corrects at ETH's higher base rate. So the class-weighted HMM would need per-symbol reweighting just to *match* the tree; **dropped as a candidate.** T0.2 net: unsupervised Gaussian-HMM regime discovery does not robustly match — let alone beat — the hand-`vol_threshold` + tree head across symbols; the frozen-edge detector + tree head **stay**. The map: off-the-shelf model classes (T0.1 embedding, T0.2 HMM) match-or-lose vs the tree → the real levers are better *data* (the wide corpus) + a *task-specific* encoder (T1.2). Offline/candidate. Design + results: [`T0.2-unsupervised-regime-DESIGN`](docs/research/T0.2-unsupervised-regime-DESIGN.md). |
| **T0.3 — graduate the conviction stacker** | stacked meta-model (`conviction-meta-v1`, already at candidate) + embeddings | T0 | `gate-check` PASS + shadow soak. **⚠️ A/B RAN 2026-07-01 — INCONCLUSIVE (label wall):** the T0.1 embedding block was wired into the `conviction_meta` win-prob stacker (v1→v2, per-symbol as-of join) and A/B'd on the BTCUSDT slice. Point estimate is positive — Δmacro_f1 **+0.039** (0.561→0.600), Δaccuracy +0.05, same sign/magnitude as the regime lift — **but n_eval=20 (5 positives)**, so it's a handful of trades, not significant. The binding constraint is **labels** (only 99 closed BTC trades; conviction's label is a trade outcome, not self-supervised). Pipeline validated end-to-end (all 99 rows got a real embedding). **Recommendation: don't chase raw embeddings on the conviction head yet** — let embedding value reach conviction *indirectly* via the improved `c_reg` regime lens (promote the T0.1 regime head); revisit directly only with a multi-symbol join + purged-CV once the corpus grows. Evidence: [`T0.3-conviction-embedding-evidence-2026-07-01`](docs/research/T0.3-conviction-embedding-evidence-2026-07-01.md); wiring merged #5299. |
| **T0.4 — probabilistic forecasting features** | TSFM quantile forecasts (expected range / P(volatile)) as features | T0 | Feature A/B; later gated for SL/TP geometry. **✅ A/B RAN 2026-07-01 — POSITIVE + BASE-RATE-ROBUST:** the same frozen chronos-bolt-tiny used as a **quantile forecaster** (6 scale-free `fc_*` cols — median return, forward-range/vol proxy, skew, up-prob, q10/q90 — into `market_features` via `forecast_path`, builder v10→v11 + producer `scripts/ml/build_forecasts.py` + candidate manifest `btc-regime-15m-lgbm-fc-v1`). Base vs base+fc on the BTC-15m vol-regime head, `time_aware_holdout`, at three volatile base rates: **Δmacro_f1 +0.023 @ 0.005 (shipped, 4.6% vol) / +0.018 @ 0.004 / +0.027 @ 0.003**, with **positive f1_volatile at every threshold** (+0.033/+0.019/+0.018). **The key contrast with T0.1:** unlike the embedding lift (a base-rate cliff that faded to +0.012 at 0.005 and went negative at 0.006), the forecast lift **does NOT decay as the volatile class thins** — at the shipped 0.005 it gives ~2× the embedding's lift. The forward *predicted range* is a better vol-regime feature than a backward learned representation and doesn't need the class populated. (First run was void — a `predict_quantiles(context=…)` kwarg bug zeroed the whole side-stream; fixed PR #5335, rebuilt fully non-zero.) **✅ PURGED-CV CONFIRM 2026-07-01 (leakage-safe):** re-ran @ 0.005 under `purged_walk_forward` (n_folds=5, embargo) + a 2nd symbol + the fc+emb combined arm — **fc lift SURVIVES**: BTC Δmacro_f1 **+0.012** / Δf1_vol +0.012 (holdout +0.023 shrank ~half under the stricter split — expected), **ETH stronger Δmacro_f1 +0.029 / +0.020** (generalizes). Two extra reads: at 0.005 the *embedding*'s f1_volatile is **flat** (base-rate cliff) while fc keeps improving it — so **fc is the better vol-regime signal**; and **fc+emb is not worth the 32 extra dims** (weakly additive on macro_f1 +0.015, dilutes fc's f1_vol to +0.005). **Prefer `fc` alone** for the vol-regime family — the strongest offline lever at the production operating point. **✅ DIRECTION HEAD 2026-07-01 — CLEAN NEGATIVE:** fc does NOT help the BTC-15m direction head (Δmacro_f1 **−0.001**, flat; emb also flat +0.004) — both frozen-TSFM signals are vol-regime-specific, direction ~martingale (~0.34 macro_f1 ≈ 3-class chance). Localizes the forecast's edge to the vol-regime family; direction would need task-specific repr learning (T1.2). **✅ LIVE-PARITY SPIKE SCOPED 2026-07-01 (operator-directed):** the `fc` head is now the **lead promotion candidate** (stronger + more robust than the T0.1 embedding at the production threshold). Serve-time question is identical to T0.1 (chronos-bolt-tiny live) → same **Option D mirror-publish** answer (compute `fc_*` on the trainer, ship via the existing model-mirror, **zero torch on the money-box**), and the forecast is *simpler* to serve (native deterministic `predict_quantiles` output, 6 scale-free cols, no reduction-seed parity concern; one publish job can serve both fc + emb). Phased path: rebuild+freeze head → mirror-publish job + parity diff test (observe-only, Tier-1/2) → candidate→shadow soak (operator gate) → shadow→advisory (Tier-3 money gate + walk-forward). Design: [`T0.4-live-parity-spike-DESIGN`](docs/research/T0.4-live-parity-spike-DESIGN.md). **Remaining:** the mirror-publish job build (the one new piece of machinery, offline until the step-2 operator OK); conviction stacker with fc (label-wall-bound). Offline/candidate. Design + results: [`T0.4-forecast-features-DESIGN`](docs/research/T0.4-forecast-features-DESIGN.md). **✅ LIVE SHADOW SOAK — fc head GRADUATED candidate→shadow 2026-07-03 (operator pre-authorized, observe-only):** the whole CPU mirror-publish serve chain is built + verified live — producer `scripts/ml/publish_live_forecasts.py` (#5476) + money-box reader `src/runtime/forecast_live.py` + `regime_bar_scoring`/`regime_shadow` merge (#5477), scheduled on the trainer as `ict-trainer-forecast.timer` (15-min cadence, #5484) so fresh `fc_*` artifacts flow continuously; `btc-regime-15m-lgbm-fc-pcv-v1` promoted to **shadow** + mirrored to the live VM. **Verified end-to-end via diag relays:** the head accrues shadow predictions on the money box (4 within 30 min of promotion, scores 0.51–0.95) and its `feature_row` carries **populated `fc_*` values** (`fc_range_rel`/`fc_skew`/`fc_q10_rel` non-zero — `forecast_live` serving real forecasts, **zero torch on the money-box**). The live per-bar scorer hot-reloads the shadow set (no restart). Shadow never influences an order; shadow→advisory stays the Tier-3 money gate. Log: [`S-M19-FC-SHADOW-T12-P1-2026-07-03`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-FC-SHADOW-T12-P1-2026-07-03.md). **✅ SOAK BROADENED FLEET-WIDE 2026-07-04:** `eth-regime-15m-lgbm-fc-pcv-v1` trained + promoted candidate→shadow (producer already serves ETHUSDT forecasts), so fc soaks across BTC+ETH — doubling volatile-episode coverage toward the shadow→advisory gate. **⚠️ RG4 first-look (live train/serve skew gate) is LOW-POWER + a watch-flag:** the trainer's mirrored shadow log was stale (03:26 UTC → only 48 labeled BTC-fc rows, 0 ETH), and on that thin sample BTC-fc scored `ANTI_PREDICTIVE / AUC=None` — noise-dominated + untrustworthy (rare volatile class, uncomputable AUC), NOT a red alarm, but the live fc head is not yet showing clean positive discrimination → **do not rush fc→advisory**; re-run RG4 on a fresh mirror + more soak. The shadow→advisory promotion stays the Tier-3 money gate (operator + a real RG4 + a head-pinned money-gate walk-forward). Program log: [`S-M19-FC-GRADUATION-PROGRAM-2026-07-04`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-FC-GRADUATION-PROGRAM-2026-07-04.md) (also records: gross-of-fees label decision; phase-3 T1.3 ranker DEFERRED — 214 labelable rows + thin EV-allocator decision space; phase-1 cost-data audited). **❌ PHASE-2 fc→SL/TP GEOMETRY 2026-07-05 — INCONCLUSIVE (not a win):** the offline 3-arm backtest (`scripts/ml/fc_sltp_geometry_backtest.py`, #5587; ran on the trainer #5588, BTC n=552, full fc coverage) added a **`real-realized` ground-truth anchor** (pnl/risk_$) alongside `fixed-resim` (actual SL/TP re-simulated) + `fc-vol-scaled` (SL/TP distance × clamp(fc_range/median)). `fc-vol-scaled` *looks* spectacular (mean_R +0.42 vs fixed-resim −0.06) but the anchor kills it: **real-realized −0.68R vs fixed-resim −0.06R diverge by ~0.6R** → the forward triple-barrier engine does NOT reproduce live outcomes (live trades close on fees/monitor/flip/reconciler exits, not clean barriers), so the fc-vol-scaled edge is an **in-simulator artifact** over a rosy-broken baseline, not a real-money result. **No geometry change proposed** — fc's validated use stays the vol-regime classifier feature (at shadow). A faithful test needs a live observe-only fc-geometry shadow-soak (exit_ladder_soak shape), not a backtest. Evidence: [`T0.4-fc-sltp-geometry-evidence-2026-07-05`](docs/research/T0.4-fc-sltp-geometry-evidence-2026-07-05.md); backlog `MB-20260705-FC-SLTP-GEOMETRY`. **✅ 3RD SYMBOL — SOL fc A/B WIN + shadow graduation 2026-07-06 (operator-approved):** `sol-regime-15m-lgbm-fc-pcv-v1` beats its base control on identical rows (ONE dataset build, `SOLUSDT/15m/v530` with `forecast_path` — feature-set-only comparison, the cleanest of the three confirms): f1_volatile **0.3957 vs 0.3719** / macro_f1 **0.5491 vs 0.5044**, purged 5-fold, n_eval 82,788. Producer `FORECAST_SYMBOLS` default extended to `BTCUSDT,ETHUSDT,SOLUSDT` (declared in `run_forecast_producer.sh`, applied on the trainer + first `SOLUSDT.json` artifact verified) and the head promoted candidate→**shadow** — fc now soaks across BTC+ETH+SOL toward the powered fc→advisory gate (`MB-20260705-FC-ADVISORY-READINESS`). Evidence: [`SOL-fc-family-AB-evidence-2026-07-06`](docs/research/SOL-fc-family-AB-evidence-2026-07-06.md). |
| **Data — wide multi-asset corpus** | ingestion of a broad context panel *beyond what we trade* (indices/sectors, FX, rates curve, commodities, crypto breadth, VIX term structure) — the encoder's fuel | T0/T1 | Long pole for T1.2; **start early, parallel**. Read-mostly store, never the money DB. **📋 DESIGNED 2026-07-02:** [`T0-data-corpus-DESIGN`](docs/research/T0-data-corpus-DESIGN.md) — the wide panel extends the *existing* as-of-join scaffolding (`yfinance_macro`/`macro_features`/`cross_asset_features` + the `market_features` `*_path` side-streams + the `ICT_OFFVM_BUILD_HOST` guard), stored as **parquet beside `trainer_store.db`** (catalog table + partitioned series) — **never** `trade_journal.db`. 6 asset groups over free daily sources (**FRED** keyless / yfinance / stooq), 10y+ history for the label-free encoder. Leakage-safe by reusing the macro block's daily-compute + one-day-lag + backward-as-of contract verbatim. Phased C0→C3: first PR = a single **FRED rates-curve** adapter (cleanest free source, completes the half-wired MES rates leg) → `corpus_path` A/B on the regime head → breadth/commodity/FX adapters → the encoder-ready `corpus_panel` export (C3 gated on the T1 GPU tier). Offline/candidate; no spend (free sources); honest-negative-friendly. **✅ C0 SHIPPED + VALIDATED 2026-07-02** (keyless `fred_macro` adapter #5381; fetched 2.4k daily rows vs real FRED — rates ~98% / VIX ~99.6% populated). **⚠️ C1 A/B RAN 2026-07-02 — BASE-RATE-SENSITIVE (same cliff as T0.1 embeddings):** macro/rates context lifts the BTC-15m regime head **+0.030 macro_f1 @0.003** (populated volatile, via better volatile precision) but **−0.030 @ the production 0.005** (4.6% vol — f1_volatile collapses; the class is too rare for the added features to help). Note: macro was **never previously wired** to the crypto head (v003 macro cols were zero-filled) — this is a genuine first test. Only the **T0.4 forecast** lift is base-rate-robust at 0.005. So the corpus's payoff is the label-free **T1.2 encoder** (threshold-independent) + a possible head-`vol_threshold` revisit — not a straight macro-add to the production head. Next: parquet catalog store (C1b) + a second free adapter (C2). **✅ C1b + C2 SHIPPED 2026-07-02/03:** the standing corpus store (`ml/datasets/corpus_store.py`, JSONL catalog + per-series) + the FRED-corpus adapter, now **widened** with FX majors + gold/natgas (`fred_corpus.py`, #5475). **✅ C3 — the encoder-ready `corpus_panel` LANDED 2026-07-03 (#5478):** `ml/datasets/families/corpus_panel.py` assembles the store into the aligned, **leakage-safe** date×series matrix (`{date, values:{series_id: float|None}}` — strictly-prior as-of, one-day-lag, forward-fill, no-backfill) that the T1.2 SSL encoder pretrains on directly. |
| **T1.1 — deep sequence models** | small TCN / Transformer over raw bar sequences | T1 (~$10) | Beats LightGBM regime heads on the same `gate-check`. **📋 SPEND SPEC'd 2026-07-01 (operator-directed, no spend):** [`T1-gpu-burst-spend-SPEC`](docs/research/T1-gpu-burst-spend-SPEC.md) — provider (RunPod spot ~$0.20–0.40/hr → ~25 GPU-hr/mo), the ledger-gated + teardown-guaranteed burst-train→CPU/ONNX-export→teardown workflow SHAPE (GPU train-only, ephemeral; zero torch/CUDA on the money-box — same CPU-serve contract as T0.4), and the **concrete first experiment** = a ≤5M-param TCN/Transformer regime head vs `btc-regime-15m-lgbm-base-pcv` on the same purged-CV gate (~1 GPU-hr; the cheapest falsifiable "does a deep model beat the incumbent tree at all?"). **Awaiting operator go/no-go** on the 3-item bundle (hard $10 cap + workflow, provider + API-key secret, first experiment). Until then: paper spec, $0, no workflow shipped. **✅ SPEND APPROVED 2026-07-02 (operator) — TRACKING RAILS SHIPPED FIRST:** before any GPU-hour is billed, the cost-visibility layer is in: a committed `comms/gpu_spend_ledger.json` (the burst workflow's month-to-date spend-gate + per-run append target), `src/runtime/gpu_spend.py` (`summarize_spend`/`would_exceed_budget`/`record_run`), a Tier-1 read endpoint **`GET /api/bot/gpu/spend`** (per-session cost + monthly total vs the $10 cap), and a dashboard GPU-spend panel — so every training session's cost is visible from run #1. **Remaining human hand-off:** add the RunPod API key to Actions secrets + fund a small prepaid balance (belt-and-suspenders on the ceiling); then the burst-train→CPU/ONNX-export→teardown workflow ships and the first ~1-GPU-hr T1.1 bake-off runs (writing its cost to the ledger). **✅ GPU-BURST EXECUTION PATH PROVEN END-TO-END 2026-07-02 (~$0.20 total, operator-in-loop):** the RunPod burst driver now runs the full loop autonomously on a rented community GPU — **ledger-gated launch (capacity-fallback across cards) → public-IP SSH with an ephemeral per-run key (zero operator config; the official `runpod/pytorch` image's own `sshd`+`PUBLIC_KEY`) → build the PUBLIC market dataset ON the pod (Bybit klines → `market_raw`→`market_features`, never the money DB) → `python -m ml train <manifest>` → stream the trained `{model_state, metrics, manifest}` bundle back over SSH → teardown (in `finally`) → append billed cost to the ledger → upload the bundle as a workflow artifact**. Verified live: run #5459 trained `btc-regime-15m-lgbm-v2` (RTX 3090, ~12.5 min, **$0.0441**), model landed at **candidate-equivalent (NOT promoted)**. Key corrections vs the spec: `build-dataset` IS a real `ml` CLI passthrough, but `train --export-onnx --parity-gate` is **fictional** (no ONNX/parity in the codebase) — the artifact is the JSON-embedded LightGBM booster; ONNX+parity are **deferred to the first deep-torch manifest** (a booster is already CPU-native). Driver hard-refuses any non-`market_features` crypto manifest before a byte leaves the VM (data contract). 6 PRs (#5448/#5450/#5452/#5454/#5456/#5458), 26 tests. Log: [`S-M19-GPU-BURST-2026-07-02`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-GPU-BURST-2026-07-02.md). **✅ (1) BUNDLE→REGISTRY INGEST LANDED 2026-07-02:** `scripts/ml/gpu_burst/ingest_bundle.py` materializes the returned bundle into `experiments-runs/…` + registers it via `ModelRegistry` at a **forced-`candidate`**, **`-gpuburst`-namespaced** id, so a burst retrain can NEVER refresh the served weights of a same-named production head (the caught hazard: `btc-regime-15m-lgbm-v2` is the live advisory BTC vol-gate; `register()` on an existing id preserves stage but repoints `model_state_path`). Wired into the workflow as an SSH-push + trainer-side ingest step (best-effort). Verified clean on the trainer (`…-v2-gpuburst` @ candidate; production `…-v2` @ advisory untouched). PRs #5461/#5462/#5466. **📋 (2) DEEP-SEQUENCE BAKE-OFF DESIGN'd 2026-07-02 (docs-only, no spend):** [`T1.1-deep-sequence-model-DESIGN`](docs/research/T1.1-deep-sequence-model-DESIGN.md) — TCN-first (vs a lightweight Transformer fallback) causal-window head vs the LightGBM regime head on the SAME purged-CV `gate-check`; a new `TorchSequenceTrainer`/`TorchSequencePredictor` whose `model_state` carries the **ONNX-exported** graph (torch is a lazy pod-only import — zero torch on the money-box, served via `onnxruntime` CPU through the existing per-bar regime scorer), and the **one new piece of machinery: an ONNX-export + CUDA/CPU numeric-parity gate that fails closed** (a model that can't reproduce on the serve target never reaches candidate). Phasing: P1 offline burst bake-off (candidate) → P2 operator-gated shadow soak + live-parity diff → P3 Tier-3 advisory. **✅ P1a LANDED 2026-07-02** (#5469 trainer/predictor/parity/`market_sequences` family/manifest, 15 tests, CPU-torch-venv validated end-to-end; #5470 burst-pod wiring, 34 tests). **✅ P1b RAN + GATE-CHECKED 2026-07-03 → CLEAN NEGATIVE (as predicted):** the armed burst (issue #5472, RTX 3090, ~7.6 min, **$0.0269**; a first attempt #5471 hit the transient empty-Bybit-fetch `rc=78`, $0.0136 — total tonight **$0.0405**) trained the TCN, ONNX-parity-gated it, and ingested `btc-regime-15m-tcn-v1-gpuburst` at **candidate**. Gate-check (purged-CV, 5 folds, ~87.6k eval bars) — **TCN macro_f1 0.534 / f1_volatile 0.162 vs the LightGBM incumbent 0.56–0.58 / 0.19–0.39**: the deep head is **below the tree on both metrics**, so **no promotion — TCN stays candidate (inert)**. A deep model over raw bars does not beat the class-weighted boosted tree on this label/data regime; combined with T0.1 (frozen embeddings, marginal) this is the **second independent negative on price-only representation** → depth is exhausted here, **breadth (T1.2 wide-corpus SSL encoder feeding boosting) is the untested lever**. Base-rate caveat + a controlled-re-run follow-up logged to the ml-review backlog. Log: [`S-M19-T1.1-DEEP-SEQUENCE-2026-07-02`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-T1.1-DEEP-SEQUENCE-2026-07-02.md). **Drop T1.1b (Transformer): the TCN didn't near-miss (~0.05 macro_f1 gap), so a more data-hungry model does worse. Next: graduate the T0.4 `fc` head to live and/or start the T1.2 wide-corpus encoder.** |
| **T1.2 — self-supervised encoder v0** | the real **"reads-everything"** model — masked/contrastive encoder on the wide panel (no labels) | T1 | Embedding lifts ≥1 downstream head vs the T0.1 frozen baseline. **📋 DESIGNED 2026-07-03 (#5480):** [`T1.2-ssl-encoder-DESIGN`](docs/research/T1.2-ssl-encoder-DESIGN.md) — masked-reconstruction (MAE) encoder over the `corpus_panel`, deep-as-**encoder-feeding-boosting** (per T1.1's end-to-end negative), GPU-train/CPU-ONNX-serve (reuses the T1.1 burst + fc-serve path), gate = beat BOTH the no-embedding baseline AND the frozen-Chronos T0.1 embedding. Phasing P0 (panel) → P1 (SSL trainer) → P1b (burst) → P2 (A/B) → P3 (shadow) → P4 (advisory). **✅ P0 LANDED (#5478)** — the leakage-safe `corpus_panel` matrix. **✅ P1 LANDED 2026-07-03 (#5487):** the masked-reconstruction SSL encoder — `ml/trainers/ssl_corpus_encoder.py` (MAE trainer: fold-frozen standardizer baked into the exported graph, encoder-trunk-only ONNX via the reused fail-closed parity gate, torch lazy), `ml/predictors/ssl_corpus_encoder.py` (onnxruntime CPU embedding predictor), `ml/datasets/corpus_embedding_features.py` (offline side-stream block, one-day-lag as-of join, neutral-on-failure), 14 no-torch contract tests + a torch/onnx end-to-end test. **No spend** (P1 is code + no-torch validation). **✅ P1b + Path-A pretrain LANDED 2026-07-03 (free CPU, $0):** reconstruction evaluator (`ml/evaluators/reconstruction.py`) + candidate manifest (`corpus-ssl-encoder-mae-v1.yaml`) + the encoder pretrained on the trainer CPU (no GPU spend — a bigger encoder was not the lever), ONNX-parity-gated, `market_features` `corpus_embedding_path` wired (builder v11→v12, #5526). **❌ P2 A/B RAN 2026-07-04 — CLEAN NEGATIVE (replicated across two corpus widths):** the `corpus_emb` block, fed into the BTC-15m regime head under purged-CV (same folds, all 3 arms on one dataset), **beats neither the no-embedding baseline nor the frozen-Chronos T0.1 emb** on the decision metrics. Thin 13-series corpus: corpusemb macro_f1 0.584 / f1_vol 0.303 vs emb 0.611 / 0.423 (loses both). **Widened 28-series corpus (#5539):** the wider panel DID cut the encoder's overfit (val_loss **2.0→1.3**) but downstream got no better — corpusemb macro_f1 0.584 (still < emb 0.611) and **f1_volatile got *worse*, 0.269** (vs 0.303 thin), while accuracy/f1_range climbed to 0.825/0.898. Root cause: **majority-class bias / representation-target mismatch** — the daily macro/rates/FX/credit corpus carries risk-regime *backdrop*, not the 15-min volatility-burst signal the head classifies, so the embedding pushes the head into the dominant "range" class and crushes the minority "volatile" recall the vol-gate needs; more corpus width amplifies it rather than fixing it. **Third independent negative on price-representation** (after T0.1-marginal + T1.1-TCN-negative) — breadth was the hypothesized lever, tested, does not transfer to this head. Encoder stays **candidate** (offline, inert). The corpus store + SSL machinery + parity gate are sound reusable infra; the negative is the specific daily-panel→intraday-vol hypothesis. **Not re-run with more width; a task-matched daily/cross-asset head (or the M18 ranker) is the only place the corpus embedding might still pay off — logged to the ml-review backlog, not pursued here.** Evidence: [`T1.2-ssl-encoder-AB-evidence-2026-07-04`](docs/research/T1.2-ssl-encoder-AB-evidence-2026-07-04.md); logs [`S-M19-FC-SHADOW-T12-P1-2026-07-03`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-FC-SHADOW-T12-P1-2026-07-03.md) + [`S-M19-T1.2-SSL-AB-2026-07-04`](docs/sprint-logs/S-M19-T1.2-SSL-AB-2026-07-04.md). |
| **T1.3 — cross-sectional ranker (revive M18 P3)** | learned expected-net-R ranker with encoder embeddings — the untested lever behind M18's OOS-AUC-0.51 | T1 | OOS AUC materially > 0.51 in the sizing-normalized allocator harness before any routing plumbing. **📋 SESSION PROMPT READY 2026-07-06 (operator-parked, not started):** [`T1.3-ranker-SESSION-PROMPT`](docs/research/T1.3-ranker-SESSION-PROMPT.md) — paste into a fresh deep-research session to pick this up; anchored in `MB-20260629-ALLOC-RANKER`. Check `MB-20260629-ALLOC-COSTCAP` (net-R label data gap) first. |
| **T2.1 — multi-task foundation encoder v1** | shared trunk + task heads (regime/direction/win-prob/vol/exit) | T2 | Long-term; only after Tier-1 shows lift. |
| **T2.2 — offline-RL sizing/exit policy** | RL / bandit policy on top of the encoder (seeds: `exit-policy-v1`, conviction-sizing soak) | T2 | Most data-hungry → last; offline RL over logged histories. |

**Honest gaps (full list in the design doc):** the label wall is *routed around*

generic and may show no lift (an acceptable cheap negative that precedes any
spend); deep/embedding inference cost on the 2-OCPU live VM is real (bounded by
the per-bar fetch-gate/budget, but compounds per head); the wide corpus is more
data plumbing than modeling and must not starve the Tier-0 wins. ml-review-backlog
items to open for the model/experiment proposals as the sprints kick off.

### M23 — Meta-labeling program (secondary take/skip head) (phased proposal)

> **Status:** 📋 PROPOSED 2026-07-17 — **PROPOSE-ONLY** (P1 pipeline already built
> this session; no live-path change). Full design (idea → the label wall → phases →
> anti-patterns): [`docs/research/M23-meta-labeling-DESIGN.md`](docs/research/M23-meta-labeling-DESIGN.md).
> Anchors `MB-20260705-META-LABEL-WALL` + `MB-20260530-001`.

Meta-labeling filters the primary strategy's own signals (take/skip), never invents
trades — it composes with the one-switch gate model and is orthogonal to M18
(selection) / M21 (P_win). The binding constraint is the **label wall** (n≈78 real
decision rows/cell); the M23 thesis is **in-distribution backtest-augmented** labels
to break it (backtest rows byte-faithful to the live feature/cost/label construction,
parity-gated before pooling — or the head learns the simulator).

| Phase | Scope | Tier | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P1 — Backtest-augmented pipeline** ✅ | In-distribution replay → pooled purged-CV meta-label eval; live-row R fix; variant-C outcome relabel. | T1 | DONE — pipeline sound; pooled edge is **base-rate between cells, not a within-cell take/skip edge** (the M18-ranker failure mode) → thin signal at current volume. |
| **P2 — Label-volume expansion** | Extend `market_raw` coverage (`MB-20260712-EXIT-ANALYSIS-COVERAGE`) + per-trade backtest rows at scale (`MB-20260530-001`) + backtest-vs-live label parity per cell. | T1 | Per-cell n ≥ ~300 pooled AND backtest/live parity holds. |
| **P3 — Per-cell / per-family heads** | Train take/skip heads scoped to ONE strong cell (within-cell edge, not global pool); purged walk-forward + RG robustness; register shadow. | T3 | Beats take-all on net-R AND maxDD in ≥ ⅔ folds incl. trend years; per-fold AUC ≥ 0.55. |
| **P4 — Shadow soak → advisory** | Head annotates matching order packages; accrue live track record; powered readiness eval. | T3 | Live agreement + net-R lift; operator promotes. |
| **P5 — Gate a live entry** | Advisory meta-head's skip suppresses the order (reductive-only, `*_MODE` flag); backtest A/B meta-filtered vs take-all. | T3 | A/B PASS + operator; rollback = `off`. |

> **Status:** 🔄 IN PROGRESS 2026-07-17 — **P1 + P2 DONE** (Tier-1, offline; run over the
> live journal, scorecard issue #6829, findings

Every perf/allocation model to date evaluated on raw pnl or risk-model R, never
true **net-of-cost R** — the gap both the M18 allocator and the T1.3 ranker named as
their blocker. **Slice B (merged 2026-07-17) delivered clean per-trade broker-truth
fees + funding**, so a true `net_R = (gross_pnl − fees − funding) / risk_usd` label
is now constructible. M24 builds that label and reopens the two parked tracks *with
the two specific new inputs* they were waiting on (clean cost labels + a within-tick
contrastive target).

Funding=0 (visibility gap `MB-20260717-M24-FUNDING-VISIBILITY`). |
| **P2 — Net-R re-grade** ✅ | Recompute the M7 review aggregates + exit-refinement matrix on `net_R`; flag any sign-flip vs estimate (esp. funding-heavy crypto perps). | T1 | **DONE** (#6808, run #6829) — committed net-R scorecard; **one sign-flip** (`spy_pullback_1h/SPY`) flagged for Tier-3 review (estimate-driven, no config change). |

| **P4 — Within-tick net-R ranker** | The other T1.3 unlock: a within-tick contrastive ranker on the clean net-R label (candidate→shadow→advisory). | T3 | Beats rules-EV in the sizing-normalized harness on net-R → M18 P2 can un-park. Same coverage prerequisite as P3. |

### M25 — ML promotion & consolidation (phased proposal)

> **Status:** 📋 PROPOSED 2026-07-17 — evidence-gathering is **Tier-1 autonomous**;
> the shadow→advisory promotion is **Tier-3 operator-gated**. Full design + soak
> roster: [`docs/research/M25-promotion-consolidation-DESIGN.md`](docs/research/M25-promotion-consolidation-DESIGN.md).
> Anchors `MB-20260705-FC-ADVISORY-READINESS` + `MB-20260628-REGIME-SOAK-READINESS` +
> `MB-20260716-PROMOREADY-EXITHEAD-SCHEMA`.

Months of shadow track-record only pay off when a head crosses its **powered RG4**
gate and is promoted to advisory (the only stage that influences an order). M25 is
the disciplined harvest: per maturing soak, run the powered RG4 eval, write the
decision packet, propose the Tier-3 promotion (operator approves) or record an honest
WAIT/NEVER. No new frontiers. RG3 (in-session CV) is necessary but **not** sufficient
— the vol-gate / M18 / M21 history is a graveyard of RG3-passes that RG4-failed live.

| Phase | Scope | Tier | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P1 — Readiness tooling repair** | Fix the promotion-readiness CLI to parse exit/peak-head shadow records (`MB-20260716-PROMOREADY-EXITHEAD-SCHEMA`); confirm `rg4_targeted.sh` runs clean; fix the frozen MES candle base if stale (`BL-20260626-MES-BASE-STALE`). | T1 | CLI parses every live shadow family; RG4 harness green on fresh data. |
| **P2 — Powered RG4 sweep** | Run `rg4_targeted.sh` per matured roster head; committed per-head TRUSTWORTHY×POWERED verdict + readiness memo. | T1 | Per-head verdict: PROMOTE-PROPOSE / WAIT (+re-check date) / NEVER (+reason). |
| **P3 — Tier-3 promotion packets** | For each powered-RG4 PASS, draft the exact `ml promote-stage` + any YAML wire as a Tier-3 proposal with evidence. | T3 | Operator approves each; rollback = demote-stage / kill-switch. |
| **P4 — Demote / retire sweep** | Heads that RG4-fail or drift get demoted/retired so the roster carries no dead weight (`MB-20260626-*` MES-skew class); decide the `MB-20260626-003` structural blocker. | T1 (demote) / T3 (retire live-influencing) | Roster all live-relevant; every stale head has a verdict. |

directions of research, I want to make sure we've done a bit more deep research
on the things we have… and make sure all of those things are planned out
holistically so we're looking at the whole picture."_ M36 is **not a new
frontier** — it deepens the open M25→M30 threads and wires them together, with a
standing rule: **consolidate-before-expand** (new input classes like M31 Track B
options-skew wait). Everything is **Tier-1 research / observe-only** until a
discovered edge clears the existing net-of-cost walk-forward gate + Tier-3
operator approval. Anchor `MB-20260727-M36-CONSOLIDATION-INTEGRATION`.

their own gates**; check on cadence, don't rush. M36 acts on M26/M27/M28/M29/M30
+ the M16 master model.)_

See ledger Study 8 + [`m30-to-m16-integration-backbone-DESIGN.md`](docs/research/m30-to-m16-integration-backbone-DESIGN.md). |

**Holistic through-line.** The four tracks are one loop: M30 discovers on the
backtest substrate → validated edges + results train the **M16 master model** and
condition the **macro sleeves (M28⊕M29)**; the macro sleeves add a **crowding/
positioning read** so entries/exits respect what other traders are doing; M26
supplies the **transition/exit-timing** defense; M27 supplies **more strategies
(scalp + chop)** fit into **existing venues**. Nothing is a new external
frontier — it is depth + integration across what already exists.

| **S-SYSTEM-REVIEW-2026-08-31** | **A real-money account was reconciling off a hand-pasted CSV frozen for 48 days, and the prop cushion the risk gate caps against was inflated by a double-counted fill** (2026-08-30/31, `claude/full-system-review-v68vcm`, session `19b15dec`; Tier-1/2 with **ONE operator-approved Tier-3**). **Two PRs: #10532 (`0dabfca7`) and #10566 (`e3c2ad71`), both merged, deployed and live-verified.** ⚠️ **TIER-3 SHIPPED: `PROP_TICKET_RISK_GATE_MODE=enforce` is ARMED on `breakout_1`** (issue #10563, operator-approved in conversation), verified authoritative on `/proc/<MainPID>/environ` (`process` = `declared` = `enforce`). It was sequenced AFTER the cushion fix on the operator's own decision — arming it first would have made the gate **AUTHORISE A BREACH**, passing tickets of $90–$120 against a `distance_to_dd_floor_usd` of 122.62 when the true cushion was 87.34. ⚠️ **ARMED IS NOT EXERCISED** — it has never CAPPED a ticket (`OI-20260831-PROP-RISK-GATE-ENFORCE-ARMED-BUT-HAS-NEVER-CAPPED`). **The cushion fix:** `reconstruct_equity` selected fills on `reported_at` alone, so on a manual bridge a close the snapshot already held was applied twice; the rule is now asymmetric (event time known → place exactly both directions; unknown → apply a loss, **withhold a gain**), because only **4 of 19** pnl-carrying fills carry `closed_at` and an event-time-only rule would have dropped 79% of the stream. Verified live: 122.62 → **87.34** against an UNCHANGED snapshot (id 19) with the SAME +35.28 fill present — identical inputs, different output. **The reconciliation replacement:** hourly `/v5/account/transaction-log` pull + `GET /api/diag/bybit_wallet_truth`, walking the window in ≤7-day chunks (first live pull `days=60 chunks=9`, 2,789 rows — the walk is PROVEN, not assumed from exit 0). ⚠️ **`journalTrust` deliberately NOT switched over**: the live figure cannot yet reconcile with the ledger's −$262.52 (windows near-disjoint — ledger 04-15→07-13 vs live 60d 07-02→08-31 = +$17.04, ~11 days overlap, ZERO at the 30d default) AND the ledger records that figure as *stitched across two sub-accounts with 99.4% in SUB* while the puller holds one credential pair per account id — `BL-20260831-WALLET-TRUTH-CANNOT-REPRODUCE-THE-LEDGER-WINDOW-OR-ITS-SUB-ACCOUNT-STITCH`. ⚠️ **THE REVIEW MANDATE ITSELF IS 25/37, NOT COMPLETE** — a third of the budget went to the two unplanned items above at operator direction; the consolidated report + ping are recorded `n_a` (deferred by operator scoping), and the retrospective + measured proposals for the skill's own review are in `docs/sprint-logs/S-SYSTEM-REVIEW-2026-08-31.md`. | ✅ SHIPPED + DEPLOYED + VERIFIED (mandate 25/37) | M15 · M39 |

Separated from the repair sweep on **two independent grounds**: timing (the sweep runs on the monitor cadence, tens of seconds) and the reason **value** (the sweep writes `stuck_cascade_recovered`). ⚠️ **The two earlier NOT-YET-OBSERVABLE attempts are now diagnosed rather than merely repeated** — the sleeve was **flat on all four pairs** and had evaluated **4 bars post-deploy, all `skip_flat`**, so no close could occur; cadence is **~5 cleanup-or-close events/day**, and **`half_open` is the likelier first trigger, not `close`**, which is exactly what fired. **The sweep was still not changed.** **Item 1.1's residue: the surviving hypothesis REFUTED, and the real mechanism found.** It was *not* `_resolve_protective_levels` grading against another trade's bracket — each of trades 4928 / 4733 / 4180 has a linked package with the correct `linked_trade_id` and `sl`/`tp` **identical** to the trade's own, so that branch is unreachable, and on those levels all three satisfy the production inequality for `'sl'`. **The classifier never ran:** `_close_trade_from_order_status`'s no-closed-pnl fallback hard-codes `reconciler_filled` and leaves `exit_price` NULL — correctly, there being no price — and `_sweep_pending_pnl_from_bybit` later fills `exit_price`/`pnl` and **never re-runs `_classify_broker_exit`**. The price becomes known; the label that depends on it is never recomputed. **POPULATION: 572** `reconciler_filled` rows, **395 gradeable** (172 no package + 5 no price **excluded, not counted**): on **broker-truth** prices, n=155, **91 mislabelled (58.7%)** against 64 (**41.3%**) genuinely between. ⚠️ **Quote the 91, not the 181** — the wider figure rests partly on `local_markprice`, the FABRICATED class. **Two confirmations:** 181/181 mislabelled rows carry **no** `notes.exit_reason_source`, a 100% signature that none reached the classifier; and the **14** rows that *did* reach it are all `unresolved` and **all 14 still grade `None` today**, so *'classified then the price moved'* contributes **zero** rows and the prior session's elimination of later price refinement **stands**. The 41.3% **independently reproduces** item 1.1's 41.0% on a differently-scoped cut — this is the attribution defect 1.1 concluded existed, named. Fix **prepared, not shipped** (Tier-2), with the `is_reduce_leg` hazard recorded and the 181-row historical relabel kept as a *separate* decision. ⚠️ **A correction landed in the same session, caught by `doc-freshness`:** the chain's **shape** was already in `src/runtime/provenance.py`'s docstring and the first draft called it unnamed — **withdrawn**. That docstring's own step 1 was **stale** — the demo branch was narrowed to resolve from the **fills store** — and is corrected here; it is the session's **only `src/` edit and it is a docstring**. **This meets G.1 from the other side:** `bybit_1` is 155 of the 181 and only 73 (47.1%) carry a broker-truth price, because `fills_pnl.py`'s 5% `QTY_TOLERANCE` rejects its netted closes — one population, two items. **Item 1.4: the size IS the margin ceiling, and the venue clamp has been inert for nine days.** `notes.margin_basis` reads `kind: venue_available` and **`position_size` equals `max_qty_by_margin` to full float precision** on both stamped rows, so the emitted size is a ceiling, not a risk number. Operator: *"Nothing is done until that is fixed."* Merged **#9700** (the process-scoped `IB_CLOSE_RETRY_COOLDOWN_S` marker; the per-order cancel + `/api/diag/ib_open_orders`, the first IB order surface that REDUCES nothing) and **#9723** (a workflow wiring gap: `cancel-ib-order` was allowlisted, validated, registered, documented and **353 guards green** while no branch forwarded `ACCOUNT_ID` — fixed, then the guard GENERALISED to derive each action's requirement from its wrapper's `${VAR:?}` markers instead of hardcoding one variable name, which is why the pre-existing guard for this exact class could not see it). **Refuted a recorded fix before building on it:** the prior session's "set a gateway Master API client id" would NOT have cancelled order 6 — that role is documented as visibility only. **Live action:** `cancel-ib-order` APPLY on order 6 → IB returned `retCode 0 "OK"` **and the order did not go away** (`PreSubmitted`→`PendingCancel`, count 5→5); the script refused to call an accepted-but-uneffective cancel a success (`cancel_not_effective`, exit 1) — the `IB_PLACE_CONFIRM_S` lesson holding on a third path. It reproduces (order 378, same state since 12:33Z). **Measured, not inferred:** Bybit is clean (5/5 non-flat symbols carry a TP leg) — the cross-venue worry did NOT generalise there; Alpaca has **13 live positions** nothing can currently measure. **Three of my own errors caught before landing as fact**, incl. an "unmeasurable" claim `impossibility-claim-guard` rejected because the tool already existed. Fixes merged across 4 PRs (#8206/#8217/#8228/#8232): redaction at HEAD + scanner `(?<!\d)` fix + regression tests; claude_bridge redaction wiring (the one unwired entrypoint = the runtime leak source) + handler-level filter (logger-only install never ran on child-logger records) + arg-type preservation; set-env refuses empty resolved values + `SECRET_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` mapping added (the gap that silently blanked the key); token shape-guard (secret-free) — hard in bots, warning-only in the trader. **Collateral P0 found + recovered:** trader crashlooped 05:29→06:54Z (~85 min, counter 389) on the empty synced credential, with BOTH alert bots dead on the same values — zero pages (`BL-20260801-TELEGRAM-CRED-CRASHLOOPS-MONEY-LOOP` files the Tier-3 design question). Rotation took 4 rounds (empty slot → tooling wrote empty regardless of paste → half-paste burned by a PTB traceback echo into a public issue → revocation actually executed); **closed verified 08:37Z**: all 3 dead tokens getMe 401, both bots active, pings delivered end-to-end, trader healthy throughout the tail. | **S-XRP-TPSL-LEGCAP** | **XRPUSDT Bybit TP/SL leg-accumulation incident — root cause, Tier-2 stopgap, Tier-3 structural fix (2026-07-21, operator-approved).** Operator-flagged live screenshot showed 23 duplicate TP/SL entries for `bybit_2` XRPUSDT (one real position, size 165.5). Root-caused against Bybit's own V5 docs: under `BYBIT_TPSL_MODE=partial`, `set_trading_stop` is ADD-only (never an in-place amend, unlike Full mode); with no leg-id tracking or cancellation, every trailing-stop tick added a new leg until Bybit's 20-combined-leg-per-symbol cap silently blocked further amends — cross-corroborated an independently-opened backlog item (`BL-20260721-BYBIT2-XRP-TPSL-LEGCAP`, opened 05:27Z). **Tier-2 stopgap** (PR [#7315](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/pull/7315), merged): `cancel-stale-tpsl-legs` system-action, dry-run default; operator-approved `--apply` cancelled 4 stale legs, verified post-state 1 SL + 1 TP live. **Tier-3 structural fix** (PR [#7321](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/pull/7321), merged + deployed): `trades.sl_order_id`/`.tp_order_id` columns capture each trade's Bybit leg id at entry (before/after conditional-order snapshot diff — the place-order response never returns it); `modify_open_order` amends the tracked leg in place via `amend_order` instead of re-adding via `set_trading_stop`; `close_open_position` cancels the closing trade's own tracked leg(s). Preserves PR #7115's qty-scoping (doesn't reopen the netted-bracket-sharing bug that fix addressed). Live-verified deploy: `ict-trader-live.service` restarted clean on the new code, `xrp_pullback_2h` back up. CI: 344 targeted tests + full CI 7961 passed. **Mid-session discovery:** the operator caught (live Telegram screenshot) that this same `bybit_2`/XRPUSDT position — open since before PR #7321 deployed — kept re-accumulating legs, because entry-time capture only tags NEW orders with no retroactive path for already-open trades. **Completion fix** (PR [#7336](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/pull/7336), merged + deployed): a new `backfill-tpsl-leg-ids` system-action retroactively tags an already-open trade with its current live leg ids. Applied to `bybit_2`/XRPUSDT after 2 cancel→backfill race retries against the position's own ~5min leg-add cadence (issues [#7338](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/issues/7338)/[#7340](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/issues/7340) correctly refused on ambiguous legs rather than mis-attribute); landed clean on the 3rd attempt ([#7342](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/issues/7342)). **Live-verified** ([#7343](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/issues/7343)): 7 minutes later, past the position's next expected tick, still the SAME tracked leg — zero new legs added, confirming amend-in-place is genuinely active. **Remaining open gap:** entry-time capture on a brand-new order placement (as opposed to the backfill-then-amend path just verified) still has not been observed live — flagged as the next sprint's recommended verification. Log: [`S-XRP-TPSL-LEGCAP-2026-07-21`](docs/sprint-logs/S-XRP-TPSL-LEGCAP-2026-07-21.md). | ✅ Done 2026-07-21 (stopgap + structural fix + retroactive-tag completion all merged/deployed/applied/live-verified; fresh-entry-capture validation is the sole remaining follow-up) | governance/M7 |
| **S-OPTIONS-DISPATCH-AUDIT** | **Options-portfolio graduation check + dry-run dispatch-alert false-alarm fix + bybit_2/bybit_portfolio investigation (2026-07-20, Tier 2/1).** Operator asked whether the Alpaca paper-portfolio account runs options strategies — answer: no, options expression is account-scoped and lives only on the isolated `alpaca_options_paper` soak book; operator chose the graduation-only policy (portfolio carries only real-money-graduated options strategies), recorded as `PRB-20260720-OPTIONS-PORTFOLIO-GRADUATION`. Investigating that surfaced `alpaca_options_paper` has placed **zero fills ever** (14+ attempts since 2026-07-07, 100% options-selection refusals from the `indicative` data feed) — logged as `BL-20260720-OPTIONS-PAPER-ZERO-FILLS`, blocking the graduation question entirely. Mid-session, operator flagged a live false alarm: `alpaca_live` (shelved `mode: dry_run` + defunded 2026-07-15) was alarming `zero_balance` dispatch failures on every signal because the per-account sizer runs before the risk gate's `account_mode_dry_run` rejection. Fixed + shipped: PR [#7127](https://github.com/benbaichmankass/ict-trading-bot/pull/7127) tags an effective-dry account's sizing refusal `dry_run_sizing_skip:` so the alert classifies it as a policy hold (journal untouched, live-verified post-deploy via `/api/diag/version`). Also investigated an operator-reported "bybit_2/bybit_portfolio Paper view shows bybit_1" concern — found no bug (live `/api/bot/config` correctly serves `paper_role`, both dashboard + Android already scope correctly, `bybit_portfolio` trades normally); unresolved pending which screen the operator was viewing. | **S-STRATEGY-COVERAGE-GUARD** | **Strategy-completeness preventer — the regime-coverage CI guard (2026-07-17, Tier-1).** Root cause from the 2026-07-16 daily review: the roster grew 6→44 but `config/regime_policy.yaml` never grew with it, so **35 of 39 live strategies traded with no regime cell** (the router fired 1 hard-gate all day). Added a merge-time **preventer** (the sanctioned structural-guard class per § "Why no new mechanical guardrails" 2026-07-09 clarification — guards structure, not Tier-3 judgment): `scripts/check_strategy_coverage.py` + `.github/workflows/strategy-coverage-guard.yml` require every `execution: live` strategy to have a regime cell OR a reasoned `exempt`/`coverage_debt` entry (`config/regime_coverage_exemptions.yaml`, `debt_ceiling` ratchets DOWN — a new strategy can't be parked in debt), AND a `strategy_descriptions.json` entry. Seeded the 35 as explicit tracked debt (`BL-20260717-REGIME-COVERAGE-DEBT`), generated `docs/strategy-coverage-matrix.md`, added the 2 missing descriptions (slv/gdx_pullback_1d), and encoded the step into `new-strategy` SKILL.md (step 6b + done-item 7). Guard negative-tested (fails on a new uncovered strategy + on debt>ceiling), passes on real config. **Phase-2 (direction-aware cells) was RUN and REFUTED for the pullback family (2026-07-17, `S-TRAINER-RESOURCE-OOM-2026-07-17`):** on the 5-year trainer feed a DI+/DI−- or slope-based direction gate did not clear the go bar (improve net expectancy AND cut maxDD) — it fails on the ETH pilot outright and the mechanism is backwards for a dip-buyer (the entry bar reads "down" = the setup, and the pullback unit already gates direction on `close > Donchian midline`). So the `*_pullback_2h` alts stay in `coverage_debt` as *measured* debt (tested + rejected), NO Tier-3 cells authored; the real 07-16 risk was correlated simultaneous alt entries → correlation-aware sizing (separate design). Evidence: `docs/research/M-regime-direction-filter-DESIGN.md`. Log: [`S-STRATEGY-COVERAGE-GUARD-2026-07-17`](docs/sprint-logs/S-STRATEGY-COVERAGE-GUARD-2026-07-17.md). **Extended 2026-07-29 (rec #5):** the per-regime net-R matrix was run for the 7 faithfully-backtestable crypto-plain debt strategies (Donchian-4h × ETH/SOL/ADA/AVAX; pullback-2h × ETH/SOL/XRP) — **negative finding, no cells authored**: the Donchian-4h winning regime flips per symbol (no common OFF-cell; per-cell n 6–14) and the pullback-2h family is already regime-gated by `adx_min` (a policy cell would be cosmetic). The pullback long-side drag is logged as a walk-forward candidate (`PB-20260729-CRYPTO-PULLBACK-2H-LONG-DRAG`); the equity/ETF/futures debt majority is blocked on data reachability → the rec #3 offload path. Evidence: [`docs/research/regime-debt-matrix-results-2026-07-29.md`](docs/research/regime-debt-matrix-results-2026-07-29.md). **Continued 2026-07-29 (rec #5, equity/futures + walk-forward → first cell shipped):** the data-reachability block was cleared by running the matrix on a **free GitHub runner** (Yahoo is firewalled from the sandbox) — 35 rows, **0 errored**, the Yahoo equity/ETF + continuous-futures (ES=F/GC=F/HG=F) path first-run-verified (#7916 engine · #7917 run · findings #7918). A reusable **walk-forward OOS-stability gate** (`scripts/research/regime_cell_walkforward.py` + `.github/workflows/regime-cell-walkforward.yml`, #7919) then graded the matrix's candidates on contiguous time-folds (`short_stable_drag`, mirroring #7915's `stable_drag`). Verdicts (#7924): **`gld_pullback_1h` trending-short SURVIVED 4/4 folds → Tier-3 OFF cell SHIPPED** (`config/regime_policy.yaml` `trending.gld_pullback_1h {long:on, short:off}`, `coverage_debt` ceiling 35→34, operator-approved #7923); `qqq_pullback_1h` survived weakly → **offered** (operator's call, not auto-authored); `slv_trend_1h` **refuted** (regime-of-sample, short +ve in 2/4 folds) → stays tracked debt. Evidence: [`regime-debt-matrix-equity-futures-2026-07-29.md`](docs/research/regime-debt-matrix-equity-futures-2026-07-29.md) + [`regime-cell-walkforward-2026-07-29.md`](docs/research/regime-cell-walkforward-2026-07-29.md). Log: [`S-REGIME-DEBT-WALKFORWARD-2026-07-29`](docs/sprint-logs/S-REGIME-DEBT-WALKFORWARD-2026-07-29.md). **Follow-ups resolved 2026-07-29 (`S-REGIME-DEBT-REC5-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-29`):** both remaining rec #5 items closed to **no cell** — (a) `qqq_pullback_1h` trending-short re-confirmed at **6 folds** (#7927) flips `short_stable_drag` TRUE(3/4)→**FALSE(3/6)** ⇒ regime-of-sample, stays debt (operator held for exactly this re-confirm); (b) `trend_donchian_sol` chop-long **faithful re-run** — the trend harness was extended to model its omitted `stale_exit_*` lever (#7926, same engine so base geometry/fees unchanged) and re-measured with it ON (#7928): the drag **collapses −9.26R→−2.32R@39** (~−0.06R/trade, the omitted lever not a cell; unmodeled `exit_head_*` shrinks it further) ⇒ stays debt. rec #5 final: **1 Tier-3 OFF cell shipped (gld), all other candidates refuted/explained.** **Vol-axis blocker surfaced + tooled 2026-07-30 (`S-OFFLINE-VOL-AXIS-20260730`):** the matrix/walk-forward line above measures the **1-D trend axis only**, but six LIVE cells are 2-D `trend_vol` cells (`trend_donchian` ×3, `ict_scalp_5m` ×2, `squeeze_breakout_4h` ×1) — so a 1-D grade of a strategy carrying one **pools vol states the live gate already refuses**, and its verdict is not decision-grade. That is not theoretical: a `squeeze_breakout_4h` trending-short OFF-cell proposal passed every 1-D gate and was **WITHHELD** for exactly this (`BL-20260730-2D-VOL-CELLS-UNAUDITABLE`, severity high). The measurement gap is now closed as **tooling**: `scripts/research/ml_vol_label_replay.py` replays the **advisory ML head** (`btc-regime-15m-lgbm-fc-pcv-v1`) offline into per-bar calm/volatile labels — resolved through the router's own resolvers, NOT the frozen `vol_detector` (whose docstring records those cells LOSE money under the frozen label) — and `regime_tag_emitted --vol-labels` grades on the 2-D cell, declaring `vol_axis: present|absent` on every run. **RUN + VERIFIED + PARTIALLY GRADED, same day (`S-OFFLINE-VOL-AXIS-20260730` continuation; supersedes the "nothing is measured yet" state this row previously recorded).** (a) **Serve parity CONFIRMED, no gap** — the replay was run on the trainer and window-matched against the live gate's own rows: offline **1.15%** vs live **1.02%** volatile (#8109). An earlier 9.1%-vs-1% reading compared **disjoint epochs** and was withdrawn (#8102). (b) **The six cells are UNGRADEABLE ON LIVE DATA at any n** (#8099) — a gate row records a *suppression*, so it has no outcome; total live n=12. Offline is the only valid instrument. (c) **The withheld `squeeze_breakout_4h` proposal is RELEASED and SUPERSEDED** (#8113): the 1-D `trending` short loss (−2.7288R/18t) decomposes exactly into `trending/calm` −3.2101R/17t (**already gated**) + `trending/volatile` **0.0R/n=0** + unknown +0.4813R/1t, so the existing 2-D cell already captures the whole loss and the 1-D cell would additionally gate a population with **zero** evidence. **No `config/regime_policy.yaml` edit required — nothing for the operator to approve, only a parked item to close.** Paper-only (`bybit_1`), no real-money exposure. (d) **`trend_donchian` graded, then SELF-REFUTED — no finding stands** (#8118 → #8119): the offline 2-D grade put `transitional/calm {long:off}` at +32.66R/n=57, contradicting its authoring comment (−$356/43t) in sign, but the repo's own walk-forward scored the **same 1-D `transitional` population, same harness file** at **−1.25R/n=94** — opposite sign. Marked `UNSAFE-DO-NOT-ACT`; **the open question is now the instrument discrepancy, not the cell**, and the decisive test is both paths on ONE feed with IDENTICAL params. **Two blocking tool defects found (both high, routed to the tooling session, not fixed here):** `regime_cell_walkforward` is **1-D only**, so the gate required before proposing a 2-D change **cannot be run on a 2-D cell** and fails *silently* (`BL-20260730-WALKFORWARD-NO-VOL-AXIS`); and its `*_stable_drag` verdict **flips on fold count** at identical pooled net-R — FALSE at 4 folds, TRUE at 3 (`BL-20260730-WF-FOLDCOUNT-VERDICT-FLIP`). Also corrected: the "vol axis reads calm ~99% of the time" claim generalised from ~979 live rows; the full 175,272-row replay reads **8.92% volatile**, ranging **0.44%–30.42%** monthly, so the volatile-keyed cells are *conditionally* dormant, not structurally. **CAUSE FOUND 2026-07-31 (#8137) — and it generalises past the cell.** Params and fees were eliminated by code inspection (identical flags; `_TREND_LEVER_FLAG` has no key on `trend_donchian`; fee 7.5 both sides), leaving the feed — which a 2x2 then split into trade GENERATION vs regime LABELLING: `A_on_A` +29.4913R n=86 → `A_on_B` **+4.5742R n=90 on the SAME 357 trades** (total `net_r` 79.28 in both, only the ADX source changed) → `B_on_B` −1.6799R n=93. **Labelling is 24.92R of the 31.17R swing (79.9%).** The feeds barely disagree about outcomes — 331/357 shared entry times totalling 67.83 vs 68.88 (1.05R apart), base rates within 0.5pp; the ~25R simply **migrated between buckets** (into trending +12.98 and chop +12.30). So the `transitional/calm` finding is **dead**, and the authoring comment is neither contradicted nor confirmed — this instrument cannot adjudicate it at that resolution. **The real finding is the instrument: per-regime net-R attribution is unstable to the candle feed** (hard ADX cutoffs at 20/25 over a noisy rolling indicator, against a heavy-tailed R distribution — a few large winners near a boundary carry tens of R across it). That reaches every 1-D regime grade in the rec #5 programme above, including the one that shipped the live `gld_pullback_1h` cell — **not shown wrong** (different feed path, Yahoo equity) but **unvalidated against a defect class the pipeline cannot currently detect**, since nothing reports feed-sensitivity. Filed `BL-20260731-REGIME-ATTRIBUTION-FEED-SENSITIVE` (**critical**): no per-regime net-R number should be quoted as decision-grade until it carries a feed-sensitivity figure. **CLOSED 2026-07-31 (operator).** The parked `squeeze_breakout_4h` 1-D proposal is **DROPPED** as superseded — no config edit required or made. **Detection for the feed-instability SHIPPED** (#8147): `regime_tag_emitted` now emits `boundary_exposure` (always computed — share of sum|net-R| within ±band of the 20/25 cutoffs, with each bucket's *structural floor* so `transitional`'s inherent ~100% reads as the tautology it is, not an alarm) and `feed_sensitivity` (`--sensitivity-data`: same trades, second feed, per-bucket delta + **sign flips**), both declared on every run like `vol_axis`. Detection is **not** remediation: the shipped `gld_pullback_1h` cell remains **unvalidated, not shown wrong**, and re-checking it against a second feed + threading the axis through `regime_cell_walkforward` are the open halves. **Session mistakes filed as a CLASS, not incidents** (operator-directed): `BL-20260731-CLAIM-SURFACE-UNGUARDED` (**critical**) — every CI guard here checks code shape, so the evidence surface that Tier-3 decisions actually rest on is unguarded; six claim-defects in one session, all self-caught, none caught by a mechanism. **No Tier-3 change was proposed and `config/regime_policy.yaml` was never touched.** | 🔄 Done 2026-07-17 (guard + register + skill DoD shipped; Phase-2 direction-filter refuted for the pullback family — debt now *measured*; correlation-aware sizing is the open follow-up); **rec #5 continued 2026-07-29 — equity/futures matrix RUN (0 errored) + walk-forward gate + 1st Tier-3 OFF cell (gld) shipped; qqq re-confirmed→refuted, sol faithful-re-run→drag was the omitted lever, slv refuted** | governance/M7 |

| **S-CROSS-ASSET-PROBE** | **Cross-asset feature probe — "do peer assets predict this asset?" (2026-06-18, Tier-1, research-framework step 1).** The cheap A/B before any cross-asset strategy wiring: a fixed peer-asset feature block extending `ml/datasets/families/market_features.py` behind an optional `cross_asset_path` side-stream (default-preserving — 0.0 + non-xa columns byte-identical when omitted, so every existing build is unchanged). New `ml/datasets/cross_asset_features.py` (pure fns: peer co-movement `xa_peer{1,2}_ret`, the lead signal `_ret_lag1`, rolling `_vol`, `_rel_strength`, rolling `_beta`, idiosyncratic `_beta_residual`, + cross-sectional `xa_breadth_up`; positional peer slots keep the schema fixed/reusable) computed at the **target's bar cadence, past-only** (same-cadence crypto peers close with the target → leakage-safe, unlike the daily-lagged macro family). Producer `scripts/ml/build_cross_asset.py` (reads peer `market_raw`, no external fetch). A/B manifest pair `eth-regime-1h-lgbm-{v1,xasset-v1}` (ETH ← BTC/SOL), differing ONLY by the feature list (both read one dataset built WITH the side-stream — clean macro-style isolation). 39 unit tests + ruff clean + builder bumped v7→v8. **A/B RESULT — POSITIVE** (trainer, `automation/results/xa-eth-probe.txt`): on a 1-yr time-aware holdout (n=8760) the cross-asset block (only changed variable) lifts the ETH regime head broadly — weighted-f1 0.556→0.610 (+0.054), accuracy +0.039, macro-f1 +0.044, **f1_range +0.085**; the lift is concentrated in the calm/range regime (recall_range 0.37→0.48 — peer state helps ETH recognize when it's co-moving calmly). GREEN per the pre-set rule. **CORROBORATED under purged-WF-CV** (5 leak-free folds, n=21,900 pooled, `automation/results/xa-eth-pwf.txt`): lift survives — weighted-f1 +0.026, f1_range +0.043, recall_range +0.062, f1_volatile ~0 (same direction + same range-regime concentration; ~half the single-holdout magnitude = the honest pooled estimate). Single-holdout caveat resolved; vol-regime *proxy* caveat stands. **ABLATION (purged-WF-CV, `automation/results/xa-eth-ablation.txt`):** the edge is broad-based but **SOL is the load-bearing peer** (dropping it loses −0.0187 of the +0.0259 total ≈72%), then rel_strength (−0.0105) ≈ beta+residual (−0.0100) > BTC peer (−0.0058) > lead-lag (−0.0022, negligible) — the *contemporaneous relative/idiosyncratic geometry* carries it, not co-movement or lead-lag. **DIRECTIONAL probe (step 3, operator-greenlit, `automation/results/xa-eth-direction.txt`):** WEAK-POSITIVE — own-features base predicts ETH's 5-bar forward sign at a coin-flip 50.6%; +cross-asset lifts to 51.5% (+0.97pp acc, leak-free, symmetric, ~2.9 SE) — real but far weaker than the regime lift. **Strategic read:** cross-asset is a strong *regime* conditioner (+4.3pp f1_range) but a weak *directional* signal (+1pp) → points to using it as a regime/confidence/sizing lens (M16 unified-confidence), NOT a standalone directional strategy. **Next (operator-steer, neither auto-fired):** (a) convert-to-PnL gate — does the 51.5% sign-edge survive net-of-fee in the readiness ladder?; (b) wire the validated cross-asset *regime* read into the M16 sizing path. Tier-3 wire NOT recommended on a 1pp edge. **OPERATOR STEER 2026-06-18: take it as the regime/sizing lens (M16), not a standalone strategy** → DESIGN shipped [`docs/cross-asset-regime-conviction-DESIGN.md`](docs/cross-asset-regime-conviction-DESIGN.md): route the validated cross-asset regime read into the conviction blend's already-reserved-but-inert `c_reg` lens (weight 0.15, "P(favorable regime)" — today skipped for lack of a class-vector/calibrator). Three gated pieces: promote `eth-regime-1h-lgbm-xasset-v1` candidate→shadow (Tier-3), `src/runtime/cross_asset_live.py` live peer-feature computation reusing the pure fns + fetch-gate-bounded (Tier-2, observe-only, kill-switched), the `c_reg` regime-alignment map + calibrator (Tier-1). Observe-only soak → M16 P2+ backtest gate before any influence. **D2a BUILT 2026-06-18 (operator "that's a go")**: `config/cross_asset.yaml` + `src/runtime/cross_asset_live.py` + wiring into the per-bar regime scorer so the cross-asset head scores with correct live `xa_*` features (observe-only → `shadow_predictions.jsonl`; kill-switch `CROSS_ASSET_LIVE_DISABLED`; fail-permissive NaN-not-zeros; peers ride the gated fetch cadence; reuses the offline pure fns so live==train). 19 tests, ruff clean. **ACTIVATED 2026-06-18 (operator-approved overnight):** PR #3965 merged to main; `eth-regime-1h-lgbm-xasset-v1` trained+registered in the production registry + promoted candidate→shadow + mirror published to the live VM; trader restarted (issues #3967/#3968) to load the code + auto-wire the shadow head → the observe-only cross-asset regime soak is live (per-bar scorer feeds it live `xa_*`). Rollback: `CROSS_ASSET_LIVE_DISABLED` or demote shadow→candidate. **D2b (next):** signal-time xa feed + regime class-prob vector + the `c_reg` alignment/calibrator (makes the inert `c_reg` real). D3 soak → D4 M16-P2+-gated influence. Scope: [`docs/research/cross-asset-strategy-scope-2026-06-18.md`](docs/research/cross-asset-strategy-scope-2026-06-18.md); log: [`docs/research/cross-asset-feature-probe-2026-06-18.md`](docs/research/cross-asset-feature-probe-2026-06-18.md). | ✅ Probe positive 2026-06-18; M16 regime-lens DESIGN shipped (Tier-1) — live wiring (D2) operator-gated | M7/M8 |

| **S-AI-WS7-PART-2** | **WS7-PART-2 — Shadow-mode per-strategy adapter.** Lands the per-strategy integration glue: `src/runtime/shadow_adapter.py::with_shadow_pred(decision, *, predictor, feature_row, logger=None)`. Stateless. Returns the deterministic `decision` byte-for-byte regardless of predictor outcome (defence-in-depth test verifies the package's keys are unchanged). Wraps `predictor.predict(...)` in `try/except` so model failure cannot crash the tick. `predictor=None` is pass-through so strategy authors can thread the helper through unconditionally. Bare `Predictor` (non-`ShadowPredictor`) is rejected with `TypeError` — `ShadowPredictor` is the audit-log surface and must not be bypassed. No production strategy is wired in this PR — strategy adoption is PART-3. Logged in `docs/sprint-logs/S-AI-WS7-PART-2.md`. | ✅ Done 2026-05-10 (`#772`) | M9 |
| **S-AI-WS7-PART-3** | **WS7-PART-3 — Wire vwap through `with_shadow_pred`.** First production-strategy adoption of the PART-2 helper.
