## Standing / Recurring Sessions

The review cadence now runs through the **`/system-review`** master session
(`/system-report` is a back-compat alias) — it runs the three sub-reviews
(`/health-review` + `/performance-review` + `/ml-review`), assesses strategy
promotion/soak readiness, drains the three review backlogs, and emits one
consolidated time-windowed report. Run it `--window=since-last|daily|weekly|monthly`.

| Session | What it covers | Backlog it drains |
|---|---|---|
| `/system-review` (master) | roll-up of all three + promotion/soak readiness + report | all three below |
| `/health-review` | pipeline/DB/service/data health | `docs/claude/health-review-backlog.json` |
| `/performance-review` | per-strategy + per-decision trade grading | `docs/claude/performance-review-backlog.json` |
| `/ml-review` | trainer + model lifecycle + experiment proposals | `docs/claude/ml-review-backlog.json` |

## Items Under Consideration (Not Yet Scheduled)

_Pruned 2026-07-11 (S-ROADMAP-REVIEW): removed items long since shipped
(M11 IB/MES integration → live 2026-05-22; the demo-PnL netting-guard Option A →
shipped + baseline 2026-06-17; the CFI auto-flatten 7-day soak → long past its
2026-05-17 window). Live near-term work is in [Next — prioritized work plan](#next--prioritized-work-plan);
the exhaustive item bank is the three review backlogs. The candidates below are
genuinely-unscheduled ideas._

**Strategy / research:**
- **`account_compat_matrix` futures/daily extension** (`PB-20260618-012`) — it is BTC/ROSTER-centric today; a non-crypto real-money promotion gate needs a futures/daily-cadence extension.
- **`mgc_pullback_1h` as a new intraday variant** (`PB-20260707-MGC-PULLBACK-1H-WALKFORWARD`) — the strongest intraday lead (+185R over ~3.3y in research); needs a proper walk-forward + compat matrix before a `strategies.yaml` proposal.
- **Degenerate signal confidence** (`PERF-20260601-010`) — `vwap` + `htf_pullback_trend_2h` emit `confidence=1.0` on every order package, carrying no information for gating/sizing/ML; give them a real confidence signal.
- **Funding-carry sleeve** (`PB-20260620-002`, dormant/regime-dependent) and the **cross-sectional rank-N sleeve** (`PB-20260620-003`, un-built) — revisit when perp funding elevates / after the ranker exists.
- **Correlation-aware exposure cap** (`MB-20260629-ALLOC-CORR`, _Claude-proposed 2026-07-28, S-ROADMAP-RECONCILE_) — the observe-only decision-time correlation feature now exists (`src/runtime/allocator_corr.py`, `correlated_exposure` / `corr_concentration` / `effective_independent_bets`). Its natural graduation is a **portfolio-level exposure cap that treats highly-correlated same-direction positions as one risk unit**: today two correlated longs (e.g. BTC + ETH) are each sized as an independent `risk_pct` trade, so the risk caps under-count the concentrated directional exposure. Gate before any live wiring: a backtest showing a correlation-adjusted cap cuts maxDD without materially reducing return (the same sizing-normalized standard M18/T1.3 use); Tier-3 to feed into sizing. Composes with M24 P3/P4 (the correlation feature is already a listed ranker input).

- **Trade prioritisation — prove the ranking key, and build the harness that can** (`OI-20260831-TRADE-PRIORITISATION-IS-LIVE-BUT-UNPROVEN-AND-ITS-AB-HARNESS-DOES-NOT-EXIST`, `BL-20260831-TRADE-PRIORITISATION-IS-UNPROVEN-CONFIDENCE-IS-A-START-NOT-A-RESULT`, _Claude-proposed 2026-08-31, PR #10544_) — **⚠️ NOT a normal unscheduled candidate: this one is ALREADY LIVE on the order path.** As of 2026-08-31 `intents._election_sort_key` ranks competing trades by **confidence → declared priority → recent 3-day PnL → timestamp → name**, graduating the `conviction_arbitration` annotation into the live election. It went live on a **correctness** argument — confidence is evidence about the trade, unlike the strategy NAME it replaced, which was not only arbitrary but provably broken for prefix names (`max()` over `tuple(-ord(c))` cannot reverse LENGTH, so `trend_donchian_sol` could never beat `trend_donchian_sol_prop`). It has **never been shown to pick the better-performing trade**. Operator, 2026-08-31: *"the confidence score is a good start, but it isn't proven that it's necessarily what's gonna give us the best trade every time."* **The blocking dependency is a harness gap:** `scripts/backtest_system.py` models ONE shared book, so neither global-vs-per-account arbitration nor a swapped ranking key is expressible — the A/B cannot be run today at all. Build the N-book mode + a ranking-key arm (the shape of its existing `--flip-policy`) and the workflow that fires it; then run the four arms in [`docs/research/trade-prioritisation-research-DESIGN.yaml`](docs/research/trade-prioritisation-research-DESIGN.yaml) — `confidence_first` / `priority_first` / `recent_pnl_first` / **`random_tiebreak` (a declared control, not an afterthought)**. **Two confounds that decide the design:** it is a **counterfactual** problem (we see the winner's outcome, never the loser's), so only a replay through the REAL `aggregate_intents` makes it observable; and confidence **saturates** — `1.0` is 20.5% of observations and contenders tie EXACTLY on **50.1%** of contests (n=371 soak, 2026-06-17→08-30), so half of all contests never reach confidence and a pooled result is uninterpretable. Stratify by `decided_by`; evaluate on the CONTESTED subset only. **⚠️ A NULL RESULT CLOSES THIS** — M18's allocator asked the adjacent question and the answer was NO (edge −$7, ranker OOS AUC ≈ 0.51); if `confidence_first` does not beat a random tiebreak, the honest finding is that arbitration ORDER does not drive outcome, and the investment should move to the confidence SCORE itself. The same harness serves the per-account-arbitration A/B, so one build unblocks both.

**Infra / platform:**
- **PnL provenance — make it first-class and enforced** (`BL-20260730-PROVENANCE-RECORDED-THEN-IGNORED`, _Claude-proposed 2026-07-30, `S-PROVENANCE-EXITLEAK-ROOTCAUSE-2026-07-30`_) — the journal records whether each number was measured or manufactured (`exit_price_source`, `pnl_source`) and **every consumer ignores it**: written in 12 files, branched on in one (for an unrelated value), **zero** references in the whole `ml/` tree. Lifetime that means 226 closed rows carrying **+$247,683.78** of `local_markprice` PnL and a fabricated share running 0.0% (May) → 30.5% (Jun) → **64.9% (Jul)**; the paper book's apparent profitability is mark-price arithmetic, and paper is the ML training signal. Real money is largely clean (187 measured / 12 fabricated). Shipped this sprint: `src/runtime/provenance.py` (the one vocabulary) + `provenance-consumer-guard` (fails when a provenance key gains a writer but no consumer). **Remaining:** give the 4 still-unconsumed keys consumers so the guard can go blocking (deliberately not baseline-waivered); `/performance` `pnlCoverage` generalizing the existing `rCoverage` pattern; the Tier-2 change stopping `_sweep_local_pnl_for_unpriced` pricing a *confirmed close* from a live mark, plus an INV-2 `unmeasured` terminal state so the invariant stops pressuring fabrication.
- **IBKR broker-truth fills** (`BL-20260730-PROVENANCE-RECORDED-THEN-IGNORED` sibling) — `ib_paper` holds **+$240,569 of the +$247,683** fabricated total. There is no `IBClient.fills()` / `reqExecutions` reader and `interactive_brokers` is absent from `BROKER_PNL_READER_EXCHANGES = {"bybit"}`, although `trade_journal`'s `exchange_fills` table and the Alpaca mapper already exist as the template. A real IB executions reader makes those rows **measured**, not estimated — strictly better than reconstruction. Separately `ib_connector.get_ohlcv` accepts no `since`, so historical IB bars are unreachable even though `pull_mes_ibkr_history.sh` already chunks ~80 `reqHistoricalData` calls.
- **Safe review-backlog append helper + drive down the 36 standing impossibility claims** (`BL-20260820-BACKLOG-APPEND-REFORMATS-AND-REATTRIBUTES`, _Claude-proposed 2026-08-20, full-system-audit session_) — the three review backlogs are the ONLY files a session is REQUIRED to edit at session end, and editing them is booby-trapped. They are stored `ensure_ascii=False`; Python's `json.dumps` defaults to `True`, so the obvious read-append-write idiom escapes every non-ASCII character and rewrites every line containing one. **Measured: a ONE-ROW append produced `2496 insertions(+), 2484 deletions(-)`**, which then made the diff-scoped `impossibility-claim-guard` fail the PR for **four claims written months ago** that the author never wrote. **Half (b) SHIPPED 2026-08-20** — `check_impossibility_claims.py --all --ratchet` grades every tracked file against a committed per-file baseline (`docs/claude/impossibility-claim-baseline.json`), registered in `run_guards.py`, with planted controls proving it catches a new claim AND catches churn at a constant total (a bare-total ratchet does not). **Remaining (a):** a shared append helper (e.g. `scripts/ops/backlog_append.py`) that round-trips the untouched file and REFUSES to write unless its own serialisation reproduces the original byte-for-byte — documenting "remember `ensure_ascii=False`" is explicitly NOT sufficient, this file already documents plenty that sessions miss. **Remaining (c):** the ratchet freezes the debt at **36 claims across 14 files** (health-review-backlog 8, ml-review-backlog 5, RESEARCH-CAPABILITY-INDEX 4, RESEARCH-RIGOR-STANDARD 4, …) but does not pay it down; each needs a `checked: <path>` naming the tool actually run, or a deliberate exemption. Tier 1 throughout.
- **Env-gate migration-survival audit** (`BL-20260617-GATE-MIGRATION`) — config-only capabilities have silently regressed on VM migration (the 2026-06-15 netting-guard incident); audit all gates for reset survival.
- Exchange Failover / Multi-Exchange support.
- Deployment automation.
- Registry concurrent-writer locking; aggregated walk-forward; per-family dataset builders; per-strategy detail-metrics artifact.

**Models (successor experiments — see the M19 phase table for the live frontier):**
- T2.1 multi-task foundation encoder / T2.2 offline-RL sizing-exit policy — the Tier-2 long-horizon items; only after Tier-1 shows lift.

## Sprint File Naming Convention

`docs/sprints/sprint-NNN-prompt.md`. AI-traders workstream sprint
plans live under `docs/sprint-plans/ai-traders/wsN-<slug>.md`.
Themed ids (`S-AI-WSN`, optionally `-A`/`-B`/.../`-FU` /
`-PART-N` for sub-sprints) parallel the numeric sequence.
M11 refactor sprint ids: `S-REFACTOR-SN`.

---

## Status Key

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| ✅ Done | Completed and merged |
| 🔜 Next | Immediate next sprint |
| 🔄 In Progress | Currently being executed |
| ⚠️ Reopened | Verification revealed drift |
| ⛔ Blocked / Scratched | Cannot proceed or cancelled |
| 📋 Backlog | Defined but not yet started |
